You are working on the PAL (Perfect Accounting League) project.

First, understand the existing codebase completely enough for future development. **Do not modify any files.**

- Overall frontend/backend architecture
- Database models and Alembic migrations

- Bank statement processing
- Ledger master and accounting mappings
- Voucher creation, approval and validation flow

{"content":" 1\t<!-- LOVABLE:BEGIN -->\n 2\t> [!IMPORTANT]\n 3\t> This project is connected to [Lovable](https://lovable.dev). Avoid rewriting\n 4\t> published git history — force pushing, or rebasing/amending/squashing commits\n 5\t> that are already pushed — as it rewrites history on Lovable's side and the\n 6\t> user will likely lose their project history.\n 7\t>\n 8\t> Commits you push to the connected branch sync back to Lovable and show up in\n 9\t> the editor, so keep the branch in a working state.\n10\t<!-- LOVABLE:END -->\n11\t\n12\t## Architecture\n13\t- PAL reads company-scoped records from the FastAPI backend. There is no seeded or sample dataset; modules without a backend render empty states instead of placeholder records. Local-only session state lives in `src/lib/store.tsx`.\n14\t- Every company-owned record carries `companyId` and pages read it via `useScoped`, so the UI never shows another company's data (backend must still enforce this).\n15\t- File exports go through `src/lib/export.ts`; only CSV (and review XML) is generated in-browser, XLSX returns null so the UI never fakes a download.\n16\t- Shared UI pieces live in `src/components/pal/`; colours come only from tokens in `src/styles.css`.","bytes":1144,"startLine":1,"endLine":16,"totalLines":16}
[trace revision=5 evidence=E0003]
[trace writeRevision=2]

<!-- message-json {"role":"tool","toolCallId":"call_fc5a91aa1a7a405eac067b28","content":"{\"content\":\" 1\\t<!-- LOVABLE:BEGIN --\u003e\\n 2\\t> [!IMPORTANT]\\n 3\\t> This project is connected to [Lovable](https://lovable.dev). Avoid rewriting\\n 4\\t> published git history — force pushing, or rebasing/amending/squashing commits\\n 5\\t> that are already pushed — as it rewrites history on Lovable's side and the\\n 6\\t> user will likely lose their project history.\\n 7\\t>\\n 8\\t> Commits you push to the connected branch sync back to Lovable and show up in\\n 9\\t> the editor, so keep the branch in a working state.\\n10\\t<!-- LOVABLE:END --\u003e\\n11\\t\\n12\\t## Architecture\\n13\\t- PAL reads company-scoped records from the FastAPI backend. There is no seeded or sample dataset; modules without a backend render empty states instead of placeholder records. Local-only session state lives in `src/lib/store.tsx`.\\n14\\t- Every company-owned record carries `companyId` and pages read it via `useScoped`, so the UI never shows another company's data (backend must still enforce this).\\n15\\t- File exports go through `src/lib/export.ts`; only CSV (and review XML) is generated in-browser, XLSX returns null so the UI never fakes a download.\\n16\\t- Shared UI pieces live in `src/components/pal/`; colours come only from tokens in `src/styles.css`.\",\"bytes\":1144,\"startLine\":1,\"endLine\":16,\"totalLines\":16}\n[trace revision=5 evidence=E0003]\n[trace writeRevision=2]"} -->

{"content":" 1\t\"\"\"FastAPI application entry point for the PAL backend.\"\"\"\n 2\t\n 3\tfrom fastapi import FastAPI\n 4\tfrom fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware\n 5\t\n 6\tfrom app.api.v1.router import api_router, health\n 7\tfrom app.core.config import get_settings\n 8\tfrom app.schemas import HealthResponse\n 9\t\n10\tsettings = get_settings()\n11\t\n12\t\n13\tdef create_app() -> FastAPI:\n14\t    \"\"\"Build and configure the FastAPI application.\"\"\"\n15\t    application = FastAPI(\n16\t        title=settings.app_name,\n17\t        version=\"1.0.0\",\n18\t        debug=settings.debug,\n19\t        docs_url=\"/docs\",\n20\t        redoc_url=\"/redoc\",\n21\t        openapi_url=f\"{settings.api_v1_prefix}/openapi.json\",\n22\t    )\n23\t\n24\t    application.add_middleware(\n25\t        CORSMiddleware,\n26\t        allow_origins=settings.cors_origins_list,\n27\t        allow_credentials=True,\n28\t        allow_methods=[\"*\"],\n29\t        allow_headers=[\"*\"],\n30\t    )\n31\t\n32\t    application.include_router(api_router, prefix=settings.api_v1_prefix)\n33\t\n34\t    # Root-level alias of the same handler, for orchestrator probes and a\n35\t    # quick `curl localhost:8000/health`.\n36\t    application.add_api_route(\n37\t        \"/health\",\n38\t        health,\n39\t        methods=[\"GET\"],\n40\t        response_model=HealthResponse,\n41\t        tags=[\"health\"],\n42\t    )\n43\t\n44\t    return application\n45\t\n46\t\n47\tapp = create_app()","bytes":1219,"startLine":1,"endLine":47,"totalLines":47}

{"content":"  1\t\"\"\"Aggregate router for API v1.\n  2\t\n  3\tBusiness modules are registered here as they are implemented. Each module lives\n  4\tin its own package (`app.api.v1.<module>`) and exposes a router that is included\n  5\tbelow, so `v1` stays a single, obvious entry point.\n  6\t\n  7\tCompany-owned modules are mounted under `/companies/{company_id}/...` so the\n  8\towning company is part of every address, not something each handler has to\n  9\tremember.\n 10\t\"\"\"\n 11\t\n 12\tfrom fastapi import APIRouter\n 13\t\n 14\tfrom app.api.v1.accounting_audit.router import router as accounting_audit_router\n 15\tfrom app.api.v1.accounting_mappings.router import router as accounting_mappings_router\n 16\tfrom app.api.v1.accounting_vouchers.router import router as accounting_vouchers_router\n 17\tfrom app.api.v1.ai.router import router as ai_router\n 18\tfrom app.api.v1.bank.router import router as bank_router\n 19\tfrom app.api.v1.companies.router import router as companies_router\n 20\tfrom app.api.v1.documents.router import router as documents_router\n 21\tfrom app.api.v1.invoices.router import router as invoices_router\n 22\tfrom app.api.v1.ledgers.router import router as ledgers_router\n 23\tfrom app.api.v1.tally.router import router as tally_router\n 24\tfrom app.api.v1.vendors.router import router as vendors_router\n 25\tfrom app.api.v1.vouchers.router import router as vouchers_router\n 26\tfrom app.core.config import get_settings\n 27\tfrom app.schemas import HealthResponse\n 28\t\n 29\tsettings = get_settings()\n 30\t\n 31\tapi_router = APIRouter()\n 32\t\n 33\t\n 34\t@api_router.get(\"/health\", response_model=HealthResponse, tags=[\"health\"])\n 35\tdef health() -> HealthResponse:\n 36\t    \"\"\"Liveness probe.\n 37\t\n 38\t    Deliberately does not touch the database, so it stays useful while\n 39\t    PostgreSQL is down and can be used as a container/orchestrator probe.\n 40\t    \"\"\"\n 41\t    return HealthResponse(status=\"ok\", service=settings.app_name)\n 42\t\n 43\t\n 44\t# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n 45\t# Implemented modules\n 46\t# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n 47\tapi_router.include_router(companies_router, prefix=\"/companies\", tags=[\"companies\"])\n 48\tapi_router.include_router(\n 49\t    ledgers_router, prefix=\"/companies/{company_id}/ledgers\", tags=[\"ledgers\"]\n 50\t)\n 51\tapi_router.include_router(\n 52\t    accounting_mappings_router,\n 53\t    prefix=\"/companies/{company_id}/accounting-mappings\",\n 54\t    tags=[\"accounting-mappings\"],\n 55\t)\n 56\tapi_router.include_router(\n 57\t    vouchers_router, prefix=\"/companies/{company_id}/vouchers\", tags=[\"vouchers\"]\n 58\t)\n 59\tapi_router.include_router(\n 60\t    accounting_audit_router,\n 61\t    prefix=\"/companies/{company_id}/accounting-audit\",\n 62\t    tags=[\"accounting-audit\"],\n 63\t)\n 64\tapi_router.include_router(\n 65\t    vendors_router, prefix=\"/companies/{company_id}/vendors\", tags=[\"vendors\"]\n 66\t)\n 67\tapi_router.include_router(\n 68\t    documents_router, prefix=\"/companies/{company_id}/documents\", tags=[\"documents\"]\n 69\t)\n 70\tapi_router.include_router(\n 71\t    invoices_router, prefix=\"/companies/{company_id}/invoices\", tags=[\"invoices\"]\n 72\t)\n 73\tapi_router.include_router(\n 74\t    accounting_vouchers_router,\n 75\t    prefix=\"/companies/{company_id}/accounting-vouchers\",\n 76\t    tags=[\"accounting-vouchers\"],\n 77\t)\n 78\t# Bank accounts, statements and transactions all hang off the company.\n 79\tapi_router.include_router(\n 80\t    bank_router, prefix=\"/companies/{company_id}\", tags=[\"bank\"]\n 81\t)\n 82\t\n 83\t# AI Provider - top-level, not company-scoped (configuration is global)\n 84\tapi_router.include_router(ai_router, prefix=\"/ai\", tags=[\"ai\"])\n 85\t\n 86\t# TallyPrime - top-level, read-only POC. The Tally URL is server-side\n 87\t# configuration only; no request can choose it.\n 88\tapi_router.include_router(tally_router, prefix=\"/tally\", tags=[\"tally\"])\n 89\t\n 90\t\n 91\t# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n 92\t# Future modules - all company-scoped, all mounted under /companies/{company_id}.\n 93\t#\n 94\t#   api_router.include_router(gst.router,             prefix=\"/companies/{company_id}/gst\",             tags=[\"gst\"])\n 95\t#   api_router.include_router(tds.router,             prefix=\"/companies/{company_id}/tds\",             tags=[\"tds\"])\n 96\t#   api_router.include_router(reconciliation.router,  prefix=\"/companies/{company_id}/reconciliation\",  tags=[\"reconciliation\"])\n 97\t#\n 98\t# Integration modules are company-wide rather than company-owned, so they stay\n 99\t# at the top level. Tally is implemented above; Zoho remains future work:\n100\t#\n101\t#   api_router.include_router(zoho.router,            prefix=\"/companies/{company_id}/zoho\",            tags=[\"zoho\"])\n102\t# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------","bytes":4387,"startLine":1,"endLine":102,"totalLines":102}

{"content":"  1\t\"\"\"Draft-voucher domain and its append-only audit trail.\n  2\t\n  3\tA voucher is a PAL accounting object. It is not a Tally or Zoho object: those\n  4\tare external posting/export adapters for later phases. The same voucher can be\n  5\treviewed, approved, exported as the client's accounting workbook, and only then\n  6\tmarked as posted after an external reference is verified.\n  7\t\n  8\tMoney is `NUMERIC(15,2)` and `Decimal` in Python. Debit/credit totals are\n  9\tcomputed from the lines, never stored as a second source of truth.\n 10\t\"\"\"\n 11\t\n 12\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n 13\t\n 14\timport enum\n 15\timport uuid\n 16\tfrom datetime import date, datetime\n 17\tfrom decimal import Decimal\n 18\tfrom typing import Any\n 19\t\n 20\tfrom sqlalchemy import (\n 21\t    JSON,\n 22\t    Date,\n 23\t    DateTime,\n 24\t    Enum as SAEnum,\n 25\t    ForeignKey,\n 26\t    Integer,\n 27\t    Numeric,\n 28\t    String,\n 29\t    Text,\n 30\t    UniqueConstraint,\n 31\t    func,\n 32\t)\n 33\tfrom sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID\n 34\tfrom sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column, relationship\n 35\t\n 36\tfrom app.core.database import Base\n 37\t\n 38\tMONEY = Numeric(15, 2)\n 39\tQUANTITY = Numeric(15, 3)\n 40\t\n 41\t\n 42\tclass VoucherStatus(str, enum.Enum):\n 43\t    \"\"\"The client's posting-status vocabulary.\"\"\"\n 44\t\n 45\t    DRAFT = \"DRAFT\"\n 46\t Never secrets.\n374\t    event_metadata: Mapped[dict[str, Any] | None] = mapped_column(JSON)\n375\t    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(\n376\t        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False, server_default=func.now(), index=True\n377\t    )\n378\t\n379\t    def __repr__(self) -> str:\n380\t        return (\n381\t            f\"<AccountingAuditEvent id={self.id} company_id={self.company_id} \"\n382\t            f\"event={self.event_type!r} entity={self.entity_type.value}>\"\n383\t        )\n384\t\n385\t\n386\t__all__ = [\n387\t    \"ACCOUNTING_ENTITY_TYPE_ENUM\",\n388\t    \"AccountingAuditEvent\",\n389\t    \"AccountingEntityType\",\n390\t    \"VOUCHER_SIDE_ENUM\",\n391\t    \"VOUCHER_SOURCE_TYPE_ENUM\",\n392\t    \"VOUCHER_STATUS_ENUM\",\n393\t    \"VOUCHER_TYPE_ENUM\",\n394\t    \"Voucher\",\n395\t    \"VoucherLine\",\n396\t    \"VoucherSide\",\n397\t    \"VoucherSourceType\",\n398\t    \"VoucherStatus\",\n399\t    \"VoucherType\",\n400\t]","bytes":13553,"startLine":1,"endLine":400,"totalLines":400}

{"content":"  1\t\"\"\"TallyPrime business operations.\n  2\t\n  3\tReads are the bulk of this service: connectivity, companies, ledgers and\n  4\tvouchers. `post_voucher` is the single write operation, used only for a voucher\n  5\tPAL has already approved. It creates nothing else in Tally and never alters a\n  6\tledger master.\n  7\t\"\"\"\n  8\t\n  9\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n 10\t\n 11\timport logging\n 12\t\n 13\tfrom app.integrations.tally.client import TallyClient\n 14\tfrom app.integrations.tally.exceptions import TallyConfigurationError, TallyError\n 15\tfrom app.integrations.tally.parser import (\n 16\t    TallyCompany,\n 17\t    TallyImportResult,\n 18\t    TallyLedgerResult,\n 19\t    TallyVoucherResult,\n 20\t    parse_companies,\n 21\t    parse_import_result,\n 22\t    parse_ledgers,\n 23\t    parse_vouchers,\n 24\t)\n 25\tfrom app.integrations.tally.xml import (\n 26\t    VoucherImportLine,\n 27\t    build_company_collection_request,\n 28\t    build_ledger_collection_request,\n 29\t    build_voucher_collection_request,\n 30\t    build_voucher_import_request,\n 31\t)\n 32\t\n 33\tlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n 34\t\n 35\t\n 36\tclass TallyService:\n 37\t    \"\"\"Thin, stateless facade over the TallyPrime XML client.\"\"\"\n 38\t\n 39\t    def __init__(self, client: TallyClient | None = None) -> None:\n 40\t        self._client = client or TallyClient()\n 41\t\n 42\t    def check_connection(self) -> bool:\n 43\t        \"\"\"Return True only when Tally answers a real company-list request.\"\"\"\n 44\t        try:\n 45\t            self.get_companies()\n 46\t        except TallyError as exc:\n 47\t            logger.warning(\"TallyPrime connectivity check failed: %s\", exc)\n 48\t            return False\n 49\t        return True\n 50\t\n 51\t    def get_companies(self) -> list[TallyCompany]:\n 52\t        \"\"\"Read the companies loaded in TallyPrime.\"\"\"\n 53\t        raw = self._client.post_xml(build_company_collection_request())\n 54\t        return parse_companies(raw)\n 55\t\n 56\t    def get_ledgers(self, company: str | None = None) -> TallyLedgerResult:\n 57\t        \"\"\"Read ledgers for ``company`` or the first loaded company.\n 58\t\n 59\t        Tally's ledger response does not always echo the active company, so\n 60\t        when no company is supplied PAL resolves it from the company list\n 61\t        first. This is a POC limitation: with several companies loaded, pass\n 62\t        ``company`` explicitly to select one.\n 63\t        \"\"\"\n 64\t        if company is not None:\n 65\t            company = company.strip()\n 66\t            if not company:\n 67\t                raise TallyConfigurationError(\"Company name must not be blank.\")\n 68\t        else:\n 69\t            companies = self.get_companies()\n 70\t            if not companies:\n 71\t                return TallyLedgerResult(\n 72\t                    company=None,\n 73\t                    ledgers=[],\n 74\t                    message=\"No company is loaded in TallyPrime.\",\n 75\t                )\n 76\t            company = companies[0].name\n 77\t\n 78\t        raw = self._client.post_xml(build_ledger_collection_request(company))\n 79\t        result = parse_ledgers(raw, company=company)\n 80\t        if not result.ledgers:\n 81\t            result = TallyLedgerResult(\n 82\t                company=result.company,\n 83\t                ledgers=[],\n 84\t                message=\"No ledgers were returned by TallyPrime.\",\n 85\t            )\n 86\t        return result\n 87\t\n 88\t    def post_voucher(\n 89\t        self,\n 90\t        *,\n 91\t        company: str | None,\n 92\t        voucher_date: str,\n 93\t        voucher_type: str,\n 94\t        voucher_number: str,\n 95\t        narration: str | None,\n 96\t        lines: list[VoucherImportLine],\n 97\t        remote_id: str,\n 98\t    ) -> TallyImportResult:\n 99\t        \"\"\"Import one voucher into TallyPrime and report what Tally said.\n100\t\n101\t        `remote_id` is PAL's voucher id, sent as Tally's REMOTEID so a retry\n102\t        matches the voucher already stored instead of duplicating it. Amounts\n103\t        and ledger names are passed through untouched.\n104\t\n105\t        Raises TallyError on a transport, HTTP or XML failure. A 200 response\n106\t        that Tally itself rejected comes back as a result with errors set, not\n107\t        an exception.\n108\t        \"\"\"\n109\t        if not voucher_number or not voucher_number.strip():\n110\t            raise TallyConfigurationError(\"Voucher number must not be blank.\")\n111\t        if not remote_id or not remote_id.strip():\n112\t            raise TallyConfigurationError(\"Remote id must not be blank.\")\n113\t\n114\t        payload = build_voucher_import_request(\n115\t            company=company.strip() if company else None,\n116\t            voucher_date=voucher_date,\n117\t            voucher_type=voucher_type,\n118\t            voucher_number=voucher_number,\n119\t            narration=narration,\n120\t            lines=lines,\n121\t            remote_id=remote_id,\n122\t        )\n123\t        logger.info(\"Posting voucher %s to TallyPrime\", voucher_number)\n124\t        # Request and response carry the company's ledger names and amounts, so\n125\t        # they are logged only at DEBUG, which is off in normal operation, and\n126\t        # are never returned to the client.\n127\t        logger.debug(\"Tally import request for %s:\\n%s\", voucher_number, payload)\n128\t        raw = self._client.post_xml(payload)\n129\t        logger.debug(\"Tally import response for %s:\\n%s\", voucher_number, raw)\n130\t        return parse_import_result(raw)\n131\t\n132\t    def get_vouchers(self, company: str | None = None) -> TallyVoucherResult:\n133\t        \"\"\"Read accounting vouchers for ``company`` or the first loaded company.\n134\t\n135\t        This is a read-only export. TallyPrime returns every voucher in the\n136\t        company's books; the caller filters by date and by bank ledger.\n137\t        \"\"\"\n138\t        if company is not None:\n139\t            company = company.strip()\n140\t            if not company:\n141\t                raise TallyConfigurationError(\"Company name must not be blank.\")\n142\t        else:\n143\t            companies = self.get_companies()\n144\t            if not companies:\n145\t                return TallyVoucherResult(\n146\t                    company=None,\n147\t                    vouchers=[],\n148\t                    message=\"No company is loaded in TallyPrime.\",\n149\t                )\n150\t            company = companies[0].name\n151\t\n152\t        raw = self._client.post_xml(build_voucher_collection_request(company))\n153\t        result = parse_vouchers(raw, company=company)\n154\t        if not result.vouchers:\n155\t            result = TallyVoucherResult(\n156\t                company=result.company,\n157\t                vouchers=[],\n158\t                message=\"No vouchers were returned by TallyPrime.\",\n159\t            )\n160\t        return result\n161\t\n162\t    def close(self) -> None:\n163\t        self._client.close()\n164\t\n165\t    def __enter__(self) -> TallyService:\n166\t        return self\n167\t\n168\t    def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc_value, traceback) -> None:\n169\t        self.close()\n170\t\n171\t\n172\tdef get_tally_service() -> TallyService:\n173\t    \"\"\"Build a Tally service for one request.\n174\t\n175\t    Kept as a factory so tests can monkeypatch it without opening a socket.\n176\t    \"\"\"\n177\t    return TallyService()","bytes":6476,"startLine":1,"endLine":177,"totalLines":177}

{"content":"  1\t\"\"\"Post one approved voucher directly into TallyPrime.\n  2\t\n  3\tThis is PAL's only write into Tally. The rules it enforces matter more than the\n  4\ttransport:\n  5\t\n  6\t* only an APPROVED voucher may be posted, and a POSTING_FAILED one retried;\n  7\t  nothing else reaches Tally, so approval can never be bypassed;\n  8\t* a voucher already POSTED returns its stored reference without calling Tally,\n  9\t  so a double click or a retry after a timeout cannot post twice;\n 10\t* PAL's voucher id travels as Tally's REMOTEID, so even a retry Tally does see\n 11\t  matches the voucher it already holds rather than creating a second one; and\n 12\t* amounts and ledger names are passed through exactly as approved. Nothing is\n 13\t  recalculated, balanced or substituted at posting time.\n 14\t\n 15\tA Tally import answers with counts, not an HTTP error, so a 200 response that\n 16\tTally rejected is treated as a failure and the voucher stays retryable.\n 17\t\"\"\"\n 18\t\n 19\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n 20\t\n 21\timport logging\n 22\timport uuid\n 23\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n 24\tfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\n 25\t\n 26\tfrom sqlalchemy.orm import Session\n 27\t\n 28\tfrom app.integrations.tally import (\n 29\t    TallyError,\n 30\t    TallyService,\n 31\t    VoucherImportLine,\n 32\t    get_tally_service,\n 33\t)\n 34\tfrom app.models.voucher import (\n 35\t    AccountingEntityType,\n 36\t    Voucher,\n 37\t    VoucherSide,\n 38\t    VoucherStatus,\n 39\t)\n 40\tfrom app.services.accounting_audit import record_event\n 41\tfrom app.services.company import get_company\n 42\tfrom app.services.voucher import get_voucher\n 43\tfrom app.services.voucher_validation import (\n 44\t    VoucherTransitionError,\n 45\t    VoucherValidationError,\n 46\t    validate_voucher,\n 47\t)\n 48\t\n 49\tlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n 50\t\n 51\t#: Statuses a post may start from. APPROVED is the normal path; POSTING_FAILED\n 52\t#: is an explicit retry of a post Tally refused.\n 53\t_POSTABLE = (VoucherStatus.APPROVED, VoucherStatus.POSTING_FAILED)\n 54\t\n 55\t\n 56\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n 57\tclass VoucherPostResult:\n 58\t    \"\"\"Outcome of one posting attempt.\"\"\"\n 59\t\n 60\t    success: bool\n 61\t    voucher_id: uuid.UUID\n 62\t    status: str\n 63\t    tally_reference: str | None\n 64\t    already_posted: bool\n 65\t    message: str\n 66\t\n 67\t\n 68\tdef _tally_date(value) -> str:\n 69\t    return value.strftime(\"%Y%m%d\")\n 70\t\n 71\t\n 72\tdef _import_lines(voucher: Voucher) -> list[VoucherImportLine]:\n 73\t    \"\"\"Map the approved voucher lines onto Tally import lines, unchanged.\"\"\"\n 74\t    lines = []\n 75\t    for line in sorted(voucher.lines, key=lambda item: item.line_number):\n 76\t        if not line.ledger_name or not line.ledger_name.strip():\n 77\t            raise VoucherValidationError(\n 78\t                \"MISSING_LEDGER\",\n 79\t                f\"Ledger line {line.line_number} has no ledger name to post.\",\n 80\t            )\n 81\t        lines.append(\n 82\t            VoucherImportLine(\n 83\t                ledger_name=line.ledger_name.strip(),\n 84\t                amount=line.amount,\n 85\t                is_debit=line.side is VoucherSide.DR,\n 86\t            )\n 87\t        )\n 88\t    return lines\n 89\t\n 90\t\n 91\tdef post_voucher_to_tally(\n 92\t    db: Session,\n 93\t    company_id: uuid.UUID,\n 94\t    voucher_id: uuid.UUID,\n 95\t    *,\n 96\t    actor: str = \"local-user\",\n 97\t    tally_company_name: str | None = None,\n 98\t    service: TallyService | None = None,\n 99\t) -> VoucherPostResult:\n100\t    \"\"\"Send one approved voucher to TallyPrime and record the outcome.\n101\t\n102\t    Raises VoucherTransitionError when the voucher is not in a postable state,\n103\t    and VoucherValidationError when it is not safe to post (unbalanced, or a\n104\t    line without a ledger). Neither leaves the voucher changed.\n105\t    \"\"\"\n106\t    company = get_company(db, company_id)\n107\t    voucher = get_voucher(db, company_id, voucher_id)\n108\t\n109\t    # Idempotency: the posted reference is the answer, Tally is not called.\n110\t    if voucher.status is VoucherStatus.POSTED:\n111\t        return VoucherPostResult(\n112\t            success=True,\n113\t            voucher_id=voucher.id,\n114\t            status=voucher.status.value,\n115\t            tally_reference=voucher.posting_reference,\n116\t            already_posted=True,\n117\t            message=\"This voucher is already posted to Tally.\",\n118\t        )\n119\t\n120\t    if voucher.status not in _POSTABLE:\n121\t        raise VoucherTransitionError(\n122\t            f\"A voucher in {voucher.status.value} cannot be posted. \"\n123\t            \"Only an approved voucher may be posted to Tally.\"\n124\t        )\n125\t\n126\t    # Re-validate against the stored lines: approval could predate a ledger\n127\t    # being deactivated, and an unbalanced voucher must never reach Tally.\n128\t    issues = validate_voucher(db, voucher)\n129\t    blocking = [issue for issue in issues if issue.severity == \"ERROR\"]\n130\t    if blocking:\n131\t        raise VoucherValidationError(\n132\t            blocking[0].code,\n133\t            f\"{blocking[0].message} The voucher was not sent to Tally.\",\n134\t        )\n135\t\n136\t    lines = _import_lines(voucher)\n137\t    target_company = (tally_company_name or company.legal_name or company.name or \"\").strip()\n138\t\n139\t    previous = voucher.status\n140\t    voucher.status = VoucherStatus.POSTING_IN_PROGRESS\n141\t    voucher.posting_error = None\n142\t    record_event(\n143\t        db,\n144\t        company_id=company_id,\n145\t        entity_type=AccountingEntityType.VOUCHER,\n146\t        entity_id=voucher.id,\n147\t        voucher_id=voucher.id,\n148\t        event_type=\"VOUCHER_POSTING_STARTED\",\n149\t        actor=actor,\n150\t        previous_status=previous.value,\n151\t        new_status=voucher.status.value,\n152\t        metadata={\n153\t            \"tally_company_name\": target_company,\n154\t            \"remote_id\": str(voucher.id),\n155\t            \"line_count\": len(lines),\n156\t        },\n157\t    )\n158\t    db.commit()\n159\t\n160\t    owned_service = service is None\n161\t    svc: TallyService = service or get_tally_service()\n162\t    failure: str | None = None\n163\t    result = None\n164\t    try:\n165\t        result = svc.post_voucher(\n166\t            company=target_company or None,\n167\t            voucher_date=_tally_date(voucher.voucher_date),\n168\t            voucher_type=voucher.voucher_type.value.title(),\n169\t            voucher_number=voucher.voucher_number,\n170\t            narration=voucher.narration,\n171\t            lines=lines,\n172\t            remote_id=str(voucher.id),\n173\t        )\n174\t    except TallyError as exc:\n175\t        # A transport failure is not evidence the voucher was rejected, but it\n176\t        # is also not evidence it was stored. The voucher stays retryable and\n177\t        # REMOTEID keeps the retry from duplicating.\n178\t        failure = str(exc)\n179\t    finally:\n180\t        if owned_service:\n181\t            svc.close()\n182\t\n183\t    if result is not None and not result.accepted:\n184\t        failure = result.message or (\n185\t            \"TallyPrime did not store the voucher \"\n186\t            f\"(errors {result.errors}, exceptions {result.exceptions}, \"\n187\t            f\"ignored {result.ignored}, cancelled {result.cancelled}).\"\n188\t        )\n189\t\n190\t    if failure is not None:\n191\t        voucher.status = VoucherStatus.POSTING_FAILED\n192\t        voucher.posting_error = failure\n193\t        record_event(\n194\t            db,\n195\t            company_id=company_id,\n196\t            entity_type=AccountingEntityType.VOUCHER,\n197\t            entity_id=voucher.id,\n198\t            voucher_id=voucher.id,\n199\t            event_type=\"VOUCHER_POSTING_FAILED\",\n200\t            actor=actor,\n201\t            previous_status=VoucherStatus.POSTING_IN_PROGRESS.value,\n202\t            new_status=voucher.status.value,\n203\t            reason=failure,\n204\t            metadata={\"remote_id\": str(voucher.id)},\n205\t        )\n206\t        db.commit()\n207\t        db.refresh(voucher)\n208\t        logger.info(\"Voucher %s posting failed\", voucher.voucher_number)\n209\t        return VoucherPostResult(\n210\t            success=False,\n211\t            voucher_id=voucher.id,\n212\t            status=voucher.status.value,\n213\t            tally_reference=None,\n214\t            already_posted=False,\n215\t            message=failure,\n216\t        )\n217\t\n218\t    assert result is not None\n219\t    reference = result.last_voucher_id or voucher.voucher_number\n220\t    voucher.status = VoucherStatus.POSTED\n221\t    voucher.posting_reference = reference\n222\t    voucher.posted_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n223\t    voucher.posting_error = None\n224\t    record_event(\n225\t        db,\n226\t        company_id=company_id,\n227\t        entity_type=AccountingEntityType.VOUCHER,\n228\t        entity_id=voucher.id,\n229\t        voucher_id=voucher.id,\n230\t        event_type=\"VOUCHER_POSTED_TO_TALLY\",\n231\t        actor=actor,\n232\t        previous_status=VoucherStatus.POSTING_IN_PROGRESS.value,\n233\t        new_status=voucher.status.value,\n234\t        reason=f\"Posted to TallyPrime as {reference}.\",\n235\t        metadata={\n236\t            \"tally_reference\": reference,\n237\t            \"remote_id\": str(voucher.id),\n238\t            \"created\": result.created,\n239\t            \"altered\": result.altered,\n240\t        },\n241\t    )\n242\t    db.commit()\n243\t    db.refresh(voucher)\n244\t    return VoucherPostResult(\n245\t        success=True,\n246\t        voucher_id=voucher.id,\n247\t        status=voucher.status.value,\n248\t        tally_reference=reference,\n249\t        already_posted=False,\n250\t        message=f\"Posted to TallyPrime (reference {reference}).\",\n251\t    )\n252\t\n253\t\n254\t__all__ = [\"VoucherPostResult\", \"post_voucher_to_tally\"]","bytes":8588,"startLine":1,"endLine":254,"totalLines":254}

<!-- message-json {"role":"tool","toolCallId":"call_008364ace39f4364b335c586","content":"{\"content\":\"  1\\t\\\"\\\"\\\"Post one approved voucher directly into TallyPrime.\\n  2\\t\\n  3\\tThis is PAL's only write into Tally. The rules it enforces matter more than the\\n  4\\ttransport:\\n  5\\t\\n  6\\t* only an APPROVED voucher may be posted, and a POSTING_FAILED one retried;\\n  7\\t  nothing else reaches Tally, so approval can never be bypassed;\\n  8\\t* a voucher already POSTED returns its stored reference without calling Tally,\\n  9\\t  so a double click or a retry after a timeout cannot post twice;\\n 10\\t* PAL's voucher id travels as Tally's REMOTEID, so even a retry Tally does see\\n 11\\t  matches the voucher it already holds rather than creating a second one; and\\n 12\\t* amounts and ledger names are passed through exactly as approved. Nothing is\\n 13\\t  recalculated, balanced or substituted at posting time.\\n 14\\t\\n 15\\tA Tally import answers with counts, not an HTTP error, so a 200 response that\\n 16\\tTally rejected is treated as a failure and the voucher stays retryable.\\n 17\\t\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n 18\\t\\n 19\\tfrom __future__ import annotations\\n 20\\t\\n 21\\timport logging\\n 22\\timport uuid\\n 23\\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\\n 24\\tfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\\n 25\\t\\n 26\\tfrom sqlalchemy.orm import Session\\n 27\\t\\n 28\\tfrom app.integrations.tally import (\\n 29\\t    TallyError,\\n 30\\t    TallyService,\\n 31\\t    VoucherImportLine,\\n 32\\t    get_tally_service,\\n 33\\t)\\n 34\\tfrom app.models.voucher import (\\n 35\\t    AccountingEntityType,\\n 36\\t    Voucher,\\n 37\\t    VoucherSide,\\n 38\\t    VoucherStatus,\\n 39\\t)\\n 40\\tfrom app.services.accounting_audit import record_event\\n 41\\tfrom app.services.company import get_company\\n 42\\tfrom app.services.voucher import get_voucher\\n 43\\tfrom app.services.voucher_validation import (\\n 44\\t    VoucherTransitionError,\\n 45\\t    VoucherValidationError,\\n 46\\t    validate_voucher,\\n 47\\t)\\n 48\\t\\n 49\\tlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n 50\\t\\n 51\\t#: Statuses a post may start from. 56\\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\\n 57\\tclass VoucherPostResult:\\n 58\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"Outcome of one posting attempt.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n 59\\t\\n 60\\t    success: bool\\n 61\\t    voucher_id: return value.strftime(\\\"%Y%m%d\\\")\\n 70\\t\\n 71\\t\\n 72\\tdef _import_lines(voucher: Voucher) -> list[VoucherImportLine]:\\n 73\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"Map the approved voucher lines onto Tally import line.ledger_name.strip():\\n 77\\t            raise VoucherValidationError(\\n 78\\t                \\\"MISSING_LEDGER\\\",\\n 79\\t                f\\\"Ledger line {line.line_number} has no ledger name to post.\\\",\\n 80\\t            )\\n 81\\t        lines.append(\\n 82\\t            VoucherImportLine(\\n 83\\t                ledger_name=line.ledger_name.strip(),\\n 84\\t post_voucher_to_tally(\\n 92\\t    db: Session,\\n 93\\t    company_id: uuid.UUID,\\n 94\\t    voucher_id: uuid.UUID,\\n 95\\t    *,\\n 96\\t    actor: str = \\\"local-user\\\",\\n 97\\t    tally_company_name: str | None = None,\\n 98\\t    service: TallyService | None = None,\\n 99\\t) -> VoucherPostResult:\\n100\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"Send one approved voucher to TallyPrime and record the outcome.\\n101\\t\\n102\\t    Raises VoucherTransitionError when the voucher is not in a postable state,\\n103\\t    and VoucherValidationError when it is not safe to post (unbalanced, or a\\n104\\t    line without a ledger). Neither leaves the voucher changed.\\n105\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n106\\t    company = get_company(db, company_id)\\n107\\t    voucher = get_voucher(db, company_id, voucher_id)\\n108\\t\\n109\\t    # Idempotency: the posted reference is the answer, Tally is not called.\\n110\\t    if voucher.status is VoucherStatus.POSTED:\\n111\\t        return VoucherPostResult(\\n112\\t {"content":"  1\t\"\"\"Learn ledger mappings from TallyPrime's own historical vouchers.\n  2\t\n  3\tPAL's suggestion engine resolves an expense ledger from approved mappings or\n  4\tapproved voucher history. A company moving onto PAL has neither, even though\n  5\tTally already holds years of purchase vouchers stating exactly which expense\n  6\thead each party is booked to. This service reads those vouchers and records the\n  7\tpairings as `AccountingMapping` rows, so the existing deterministic chain\n  8\tanswers from the company's real history instead of reporting\n  9\t`NO_HISTORICAL_MAPPING`.\n 10\t\n 11\tWhat makes this evidence and not a guess:\n 12\t\n 13\t* only Tally's own posted vouchers are read, never PAL drafts;\n 14\t* a voucher contributes only when its party line and exactly one non-tax\n 15\t  expense line can both be resolved in PAL's synced ledger master; and\n 16\t* a party whose history is split across heads with no clear majority is\n 17\t  skipped, so an ambiguous party stays a reviewer's decision.\n 18\t\n 19\tThe read is read-only. PAL never writes to TallyPrime.\n 20\t\"\"\"\n 21\t\n 22\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n 23\t\n 24\timport uuid\n 25\tfrom collections import Counter, defaultdict\n 26\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n 27\tfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\n 28\t\n 29\tfrom sqlalchemy import or_, select\n 30\tfrom sqlalchemy.orm import Session\n 31\t\n 32\tfrom app.integrations.tally import TallyError, TallyService, get_tally_service\n 33\tfrom app.models.accounting_mapping import (\n 34\t    TALLY_HISTORY_CONTEXT,\n 35\t    AccountingMapping,\n 36\t    AccountingMappingMatchType,\n 37\t    AccountingMappingSourceType,\n 38\t)\n 39\tfrom app.models.ledger import Ledger\n 40\tfrom app.models.vendor import Vendor\n 41\tfrom app.services.company import get_company\n 42\tfrom app.services.ledger import is_tax_or_adjustment_ledger\n 43\t\n 44\t#: Groups holding party ledgers, by voucher direction.\n 45\t_CREDITOR_GROUPS = {\"sundry creditors\"}\n 46\t_DEBTOR_GROUPS = {\"sundry debtors\"}\n 47\t\n 48\t#: Groups that are never an expense/income head even when debited.\n 49\t_NON_EXPENSE_GROUPS = (\n 50\t    _CREDITOR_GROUPS | _DEBTOR_GROUPS | {\"bank accounts\", \"cash-in-hand\", \"cash in hand\"}\n 51\t)\n 52\t\n 53\t#: Tax, withholding and rounding lines are never the accounting head. The\n 54\t#: predicate lives in `app.services.ledger` so the AI picker applies the same\n 55\t#: rule when it narrows its candidates.\n 56\t_is_tax_or_adjustment = is_tax_or_adjustment_ledger\n 57\t\n 58\t#: A party must use one head in more than this share of its vouchers before the\n 59\t#: mapping is recorded. Below it the party genuinely spans several heads.\n 60\t_MIN_MAJORITY = 0.6\n 61\t\n 62\t#: Fewest vouchers a party needs before its history counts as evidence.\n 63\t_MIN_VOUCHERS = 1\n 64\t\n 65\t#: Stamped on `transaction_context` so a later run can tell its own rows apart\n 66\t#: from a mapping a person confirmed. Only rows carrying this are rewritten.\n 67\t_TALLY_PROVENANCE = TALLY_HISTORY_CONTEXT\n 68\t\n 69\t\n 70\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n 71\tclass TallyMappingSyncResult:\n 72\t    \"\"\"Outcome of one mapping-learning run.\"\"\"\n 73\t\n 74\t    success: bool\n 75\t    company_id: uuid.UUID\n 76\t    tally_company_name: str\n 77\t    vouchers_fetched: int\n 78\t    vouchers_used: int\n 79\t    parties_seen: int\n 80\t    mappings_created: int\n 81\t    mappings_updated: int\n 82\t    mappings_skipped: int\n 83\t    mappings_preserved: int\n 84\t    error_message: str | None\n 85\t\n 86\t\n 87\tdef _group_of(ledger: Ledger) -> str:\n 88\t    return (ledger.parent_group or \"\").strip().lower()\n 89\t\n 90\t\n 91\tdef _is_purchase(voucher_type: str | None) -> bool:\n 92\t    return \"purchase\" in (voucher_type or \"\").strip().lower()\n 93\t\n 94\t\n 95\tdef _is_sales(voucher_type: str | None) -> bool:\n 96\t    lowered = (voucher_type or \"\").strip().lower()\n 97\t    return \"sales\" in lowered and \"return\" not in lowered\n 98\t\n 99\t\n100\tdef _ledger_index(db: Session, company_id: uuid.UUID) -> dict[str, Ledger]:\n101\t    ledgers = db.scalars(\n102\t        select(Ledger).where(\n103\t            Ledger.company_id == company_id, Ledger.is_active.is_(True)\n104\t        )\n105\t    ).all()\n106\t    return {ledger.name.strip().lower(): ledger for ledger in ledgers}\n107\t\n108\t\n109\tdef _party_and_head(\n110\t    voucher, index: dict[str, Ledger], *, is_sales: bool\n111\t) -> tuple[Ledger, Ledger] | None:\n112\t    \"\"\"Return (party ledger, expense/income ledger) for one Tally voucher.\n113\t\n114\t    On a purchase the party is credited and the expense debited; a sales\n115\t    voucher is the mirror image. Anything ambiguous returns None.\n116\t    \"\"\"\n117\t    party_groups = _DEBTOR_GROUPS if is_sales else _CREDITOR_GROUPS\n118\t    party: Ledger | None = None\n119\t    heads: list[Ledger] = []\n120\t\n121\t    for entry in voucher.entries:\n122\t        ledger = index.get((entry.ledger_name or \"\").strip().lower())\n123\t        if ledger is None:\n124\t            continue\n125\t        group = _group_of(ledger)\n126\t        # is_deemed_positive marks the ledger Tally debited.\n127\t        debited = entry.is_deemed_positive\n128\t        if group in party_groups and debited is is_sales:\n129\t            if party is not None and party.id != ledger.id:\n130\t                return None\n131\t            party = ledger\n132\t        elif (\n133\t            group not in _NON_EXPENSE_GROUPS\n134\t            and not _is_tax_or_adjustment(ledger)\n135\t            and debited is not is_sales\n136\t        ):\n137\t            heads.append(ledger)\n138\t\n139\t    if party is None:\n140\t        return None\n141\t    distinct = {ledger.id: ledger for ledger in heads}\n142\t    if len(distinct) != 1:\n143\t        return None\n144\t    return party, next(iter(distinct.values()))\n145\t\n146\t\n147\tdef _vendor_for_party(\n148\t    db: Session, company_id: uuid.UUID, party_name: str\n149\t) -> Vendor | None:\n150\t    \"\"\"Find the PAL vendor whose creditor ledger or name is this Tally party.\"\"\"\n151\t    lowered = party_name.strip().lower()\n152\t    vendors = db.scalars(select(Vendor).where(Vendor.company_id == company_id)).all()\n153\t    for vendor in vendors:\n154\t        if (vendor.creditor_ledger or \"\").strip().lower() == lowered:\n155\t            return vendor\n156\t    for vendor in vendors:\n157\t        if vendor.name.strip().lower() == lowered:\n158\t            return vendor\n159\t    return None\n160\t\n161\t\n162\tdef sync_tally_accounting_mappings(\n163\t    db: Session,\n164\t    company_id: uuid.UUID,\n165\t    tally_company_name: str,\n166\t    *,\n167\t    actor: str = \"local-user\",\n168\t    service: TallyService | None = None,\n169\t) -> TallyMappingSyncResult:\n170\t    \"\"\"Record ledger mappings learned from Tally's historical vouchers.\n171\t\n172\t    Requires the ledger master to be synced first: a voucher whose ledgers are\n173\t    not in PAL's master cannot contribute. Raises CompanyNotFoundError if the\n174\t    company does not exist.\n175\t    \"\"\"\n176\t    get_company(db, company_id)\n177\t    tally_company_name = tally_company_name.strip()\n178\t    owned_service = service is None\n179\t    svc: TallyService = service or get_tally_service()\n180\t\n181\t    try:\n182\t        result = svc.get_vouchers(tally_company_name)\n183\t    except TallyError as exc:\n184\t        return TallyMappingSyncResult(\n185\t            success=False,\n186\t            company_id=company_id,\n187\t            tally_company_name=tally_company_name,\n188\t            vouchers_fetched=0,\n189\t            vouchers_used=0,\n190\t            parties_seen=0,\n191\t            mappings_created=0,\n192\t            mappings_updated=0,\n193\t            mappings_skipped=0,\n194\t            mappings_preserved=0,\n195\t            error_message=str(exc),\n196\t        )\n197\t    finally:\n198\t        if owned_service:\n199\t            svc.close()\n200\t\n201\t    index = _ledger_index(db, company_id)\n202\t    # (party ledger id, voucher kind) -> Counter of head ledger ids\n203\t    observed: dict[tuple[uuid.UUID, str], Counter] = defaultdict(Counter)\n204\t    party_names: dict[uuid.UUID, str] = {}\n205\t    used = 0\n206\t\n207\t    for voucher in result.vouchers:\n208\t        is_sales = _is_sales(voucher.voucher_type)\n209\t        if not is_sales and not _is_purchase(voucher.voucher_type):\n210\t            continue\n211\t        pair = _party_and_head(voucher, index, is_sales=is_sales)\n212\t        if pair is None:\n213\t            continue\n214\t        party, head = pair\n215\t        observed[(party.id, \"SALES\" if is_sales else \"PURCHASE\")][head.id] += 1\n216\t        party_names[party.id] = party.name\n217\t        used += 1\n218\t\n219\t    created = updated = skipped = preserved = 0\n220\t    now = datetime.now(tz=timezone.utc)\n221\t\n222\t    for (party_id, kind), counts in observed.items():\n223\t        total = sum(counts.values())\n224\t        head_id, hits = counts.most_common(1)[0]\n225\t        if total < _MIN_VOUCHERS or hits / total < _MIN_MAJORITY:\n226\t            skipped += 1\n227\t            continue\n228\t\n229\t        party_name = party_names[party_id]\n230\t        vendor = _vendor_for_party(db, company_id, party_name)\n231\t        head = db.get(Ledger, head_id)\n232\t        if head is None:\n233\t            skipped += 1\n234\t            continue\n235\t\n236\t        reason = (\n237\t            f\"Tally history: {hits} of {total} {kind.lower()} voucher(s) for \"\n238\t            f\"'{party_name}' used '{head.name}'.\"\n239\t        )\n240\t        # Match on either identity a mapping may carry: a manual row created\n241\t        # through the API often has only vendor_id, this service's own rows\n242\t        # carry the party name. Missing one would add a second active mapping\n243\t        # for the same party and make the suggestion AMBIGUOUS.\n244\t        identity = [AccountingMapping.counterparty_name == party_name]\n245\t        if vendor is not None:\n246\t            identity.append(AccountingMapping.vendor_id == vendor.id)\n247\t        existing = db.scalar(\n248\t            select(AccountingMapping).where(\n249\t                AccountingMapping.company_id == company_id,\n250\t                AccountingMapping.source_type\n251\t                == AccountingMappingSourceType.INVOICE,\n252\t                AccountingMapping.voucher_type == kind,\n253\t                or_(*identity),\n254\t            )\n255\t        )\n256\t        if existing is not None and existing.transaction_context != _TALLY_PROVENANCE:\n257\t            # A person confirmed this one. Tally history never overrides it.\n258\t            preserved += 1\n259\t            continue\n260\t        if existing is None:\n261\t            db.add(\n262\t                AccountingMapping(\n263\t                    company_id=company_id,\n264\t                    source_type=AccountingMappingSourceType.INVOICE,\n265\t                    match_type=(\n266\t                        AccountingMappingMatchType.VENDOR\n267\t                        if vendor is not None\n268\t                        else AccountingMappingMatchType.COUNTERPARTY\n269\t                    ),\n270\t                    vendor_id=vendor.id if vendor is not None else None,\n271\t                    counterparty_name=party_name,\n272\t                    transaction_context=_TALLY_PROVENANCE,\n273\t                    voucher_type=kind,\n274\t                    ledger_id=head.id,\n275\t                    reason=reason,\n276\t                    approved_by=actor,\n277\t                    approved_at=now,\n278\t                    is_active=True,\n279\t                )\n280\t            )\n281\t            created += 1\n282\t        elif existing.ledger_id != head.id or existing.reason != reason:\n283\t            existing.ledger_id = head.id\n284\t            existing.reason = reason\n285\t            existing.vendor_id = vendor.id if vendor is not None else None\n286\t            existing.match_type = (\n287\t                AccountingMappingMatchType.VENDOR\n288\t                if vendor is not None\n289\t                else AccountingMappingMatchType.COUNTERPARTY\n290\t            )\n291\t            existing.is_active = True\n292\t            existing.approved_by = actor\n293\t            existing.approved_at = <!-- message-json {"role":"tool","toolCallId":"call_cabdf5aa27954a6da5b8dae5","content":"{\"content\":\"  1\\t\\\"\\\"\\\"Learn ledger mappings from TallyPrime's own historical vouchers.\\n  2\\t\\n  3\\tPAL's suggestion engine resolves an expense ledger from approved mappings or\\n  4\\tapproved voucher history. A company moving onto PAL has neither, even though\\n  5\\tTally already holds years of purchase vouchers stating exactly which expense\\n  6\\thead each party is booked to. This service reads those vouchers and records the\\n  7\\tpairings as `AccountingMapping` rows, so the existing deterministic chain\\n  8\\tanswers from the company's real history instead of reporting\\n  9\\t`NO_HISTORICAL_MAPPING`.\\n 10\\t\\n 11\\tWhat makes this evidence and not a guess:\\n 12\\t\\n 13\\t* only Tally's own posted vouchers are read, never PAL drafts;\\n 14\\t* a voucher contributes only when its party line and exactly one non-tax\\n 15\\t  expense line can both be resolved in PAL's synced ledger master; and\\n 16\\t* a party whose history is split across heads with no clear majority is\\n 17\\t  skipped, so an ambiguous party stays a reviewer's decision.\\n 18\\t\\n 19\\tThe read is read-only. PAL never writes to TallyPrime.\\n 20\\t\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n 21\\t\\n 22\\tfrom __future__ import annotations\\n 23\\t\\n 24\\timport uuid\\n 25\\tfrom collections import Counter, defaultdict\\n 26\\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\\n 27\\tfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\\n 28\\t\\n 29\\tfrom sqlalchemy import or_, select\\n 30\\tfrom sqlalchemy.orm import Session\\n 31\\t\\n 32\\tfrom app.integrations.tally import TallyError, TallyService, get_tally_service\\n 33\\tfrom app.models.accounting_mapping import (\\n 34\\t    TALLY_HISTORY_CONTEXT,\\n 35\\t    AccountingMapping,\\n 36\\t    AccountingMappingMatchType,\\n 37\\t    AccountingMappingSourceType,\\n 38\\t)\\n 39\\tfrom app.models.ledger import Ledger\\n 40\\tfrom app.models.vendor import Vendor\\n 41\\tfrom app.services.company import get_company\\n 42\\tfrom app.services.ledger import is_tax_or_adjustment_ledger\\n 43\\t\\n 44\\t#: Groups holding party ledgers, by voucher direction.\\n 45\\t_CREDITOR_GROUPS = {\\\"sundry creditors\\\"}\\n 46\\t_DEBTOR_GROUPS = {\\\"sundry debtors\\\"}\\n 47\\t\\n 48\\t#: Groups that are never an expense/income head even when debited.\\n 49\\t_NON_EXPENSE_GROUPS = (\\n 50\\t    _CREDITOR_GROUPS | _DEBTOR_GROUPS | {\\\"bank accounts\\\", \\\"cash-in-hand\\\", \\\"cash in hand\\\"}\\n 51\\t)\\n 52\\t\\n 53\\t#: Tax, withholding and rounding lines are never the accounting head. The\\n 54\\t#: predicate lives in `app.services.ledger` so the AI picker applies the same\\n 55\\t#: rule when it narrows its candidates.\\n 56\\t_is_tax_or_adjustment = is_tax_or_adjustment_ledger\\n 57\\t\\n 58\\t#: A party must use one head in more than this share of its vouchers before the\\n 59\\t#: mapping is recorded. Below it the party genuinely spans several heads.\\n 60\\t_MIN_MAJORITY = 0.6\\n 61\\t\\n 62\\t#: Fewest vouchers a party needs before its history counts as evidence.\\n 63\\t_MIN_VOUCHERS = 1\\n 64\\t\\n 65\\t#: Stamped on `transaction_context` so a later run can tell its own rows apart\\n 66\\t#: from a mapping a person confirmed. Only rows carrying this are rewritten.\\n 67\\t_TALLY_PROVENANCE = TALLY_HISTORY_CONTEXT\\n 68\\t\\n 69\\t\\n 70\\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\\n 71\\tclass TallyMappingSyncResult:\\n 72\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"Outcome of one mapping-learning run.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n 73\\t\\n 74\\t    success: bool\\n 75\\t    company_id: uuid.UUID\\n 76\\t    tally_company_name: str\\n 77\\t    vouchers_fetched: int\\n 78\\t    vouchers_used: int\\n 79\\t    parties_seen: int\\n 80\\t    mappings_created: int\\n 81\\t    mappings_updated: int\\n 82\\t    mappings_skipped: int\\n 83\\t    mappings_preserved: int\\n 84\\t    error_message: str | None\\n 85\\t\\n 86\\t\\n 87\\tdef _group_of(ledger: Ledger) -> str:\\n 88\\t    return (ledger.parent_group or \\\"\\\").strip().lower()\\n 89\\t\\n 90\\t\\n 91\\tdef _is_purchase(voucher_type: str | None) -> bool:\\n 92\\t    return \\\"purchase\\\" in (voucher_type or \\\"\\\").strip().lower()\\n 93\\t\\n 94\\t\\n 95\\tdef _is_sales(voucher_type: str | None) -> bool:\\n 96\\t    lowered = (voucher_type or \\\"\\\").strip().lower()\\n 97\\t    return \\\"sales\\\" in lowered and \\\"return\\\" not in lowered\\n 98\\t\\n 99\\t\\n100\\tdef _ledger_index(db: Session, company_id: uuid.UUID) -> dict[str, Ledger]:\\n101\\t    ledgers = db.scalars(\\n102\\t        select(Ledger).where(\\n103\\t            Ledger.company_id == company_id, Ledger.is_active.is_(True)\\n104\\t        )\\n105\\t    ).all()\\n106\\t    return {ledger.name.strip().lower(): ledger for ledger in ledgers}\\n107\\t\\n108\\t\\n109\\tdef _party_and_head(\\n110\\t    voucher, index: dict[str, Ledger], *, is_sales: bool\\n111\\t) -> tuple[Ledger, Ledger] | None:\\n112\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"Return (party ledger, expense/income ledger) for one Tally voucher.\\n113\\t\\n114\\t    On a purchase the party is credited and the expense debited; a sales\\n115\\t    voucher is the mirror image. Anything ambiguous returns None.\\n116\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n117\\t    party_groups = _DEBTOR_GROUPS if is_sales else _CREDITOR_GROUPS\\n118\\t    party: Ledger | None = None\\n119\\t    heads: list[Ledger] = []\\n120\\t\\n121\\t    for entry in voucher.entries:\\n122\\t        ledger = index.get((entry.ledger_name or \\\"\\\").strip().lower())\\n123\\t        if ledger is None:\\n124\\t            continue\\n125\\t        group = _group_of(ledger)\\n126\\t        # is_deemed_positive marks the ledger Tally debited.\\n127\\t        debited = entry.is_deemed_positive\\n128\\t        if group in party_groups and debited is is_sales:\\n129\\t            if party is not None and party.id != ledger.id:\\n130\\t                return None\\n131\\t            party = ledger\\n132\\t        elif (\\n133\\t            group not in _NON_EXPENSE_GROUPS\\n134\\t            and not _is_tax_or_adjustment(ledger)\\n135\\t            and debited is not is_sales\\n136\\t        ):\\n137\\t            heads.append(ledger)\\n138\\t\\n139\\t    if party is None:\\n140\\t        return None\\n141\\t    distinct = {ledger.id: ledger for ledger in heads}\\n142\\t    if len(distinct) != 1:\\n143\\t        return None\\n144\\t    return party, next(iter(distinct.values()))\\n145\\t\\n146\\t\\n147\\tdef _vendor_for_party(\\n148\\t    db: Session, company_id: uuid.UUID, party_name: str\\n149\\t) -> Vendor | None:\\n150\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"Find the PAL vendor whose creditor ledger or name is this Tally party.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n151\\t    lowered = party_name.strip().lower()\\n152\\t    vendors = db.scalars(select(Vendor).where(Vendor.company_id == company_id)).all()\\n153\\t    for vendor in vendors:\\n154\\t        if (vendor.creditor_ledger or \\\"\\\").strip().lower() == lowered:\\n155\\t            return vendor\\n156\\t    for vendor in vendors:\\n157\\t        if vendor.name.strip().lower() == lowered:\\n158\\t            return vendor\\n159\\t    return None\\n160\\t\\n161\\t\\n162\\tdef sync_tally_accounting_mappings(\\n163\\t    db: Session,\\n164\\t    company_id: uuid.UUID,\\n165\\t    tally_company_name: str,\\n166\\t    *,\\n167\\t    actor: str = \\\"local-user\\\",\\n168\\t    service: TallyService | None = None,\\n169\\t) -> TallyMappingSyncResult:\\n170\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"Record ledger mappings learned from Tally's historical vouchers.\\n171\\t\\n172\\t    Requires the ledger master to be synced first: a voucher whose ledgers are\\n173\\t    not in PAL's master cannot contribute. Raises CompanyNotFoundError if the\\n174\\t    company does not exist.\\n175\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n176\\t    get_company(db, company_id)\\n177\\t    tally_company_name = tally_company_name.strip()\\n178\\t    owned_service = service is None\\n179\\t    svc: TallyService = service or get_tally_service()\\n180\\t\\n181\\t    try:\\n182\\t        result = svc.get_vouchers(tally_company_name)\\n183\\t    except TallyError as exc:\\n184\\t        return TallyMappingSyncResult(\\n185\\t            success=False,\\n186\\t            company_id=company_id,\\n187\\t            tally_company_name=tally_company_name,\\n188\\t            vouchers_fetched=0,\\n189\\t            vouchers_used=0,\\n190\\t            parties_seen=0,\\n191\\t            mappings_created=0,\\n192\\t            mappings_updated=0,\\n193\\t            mappings_skipped=0,\\n194\\t            mappings_preserved=0,\\n195\\t            error_message=str(exc),\\n196\\t        )\\n197\\t    finally:\\n198\\t        if owned_service:\\n199\\t            svc.close()\\n200\\t\\n201\\t    index = _ledger_index(db, company_id)\\n202\\t    # (party ledger id, voucher kind) -> Counter of head ledger ids\\n203\\t    observed: dict[tuple[uuid.UUID, str], Counter] = defaultdict(Counter)\\n204\\t    party_names: dict[uuid.UUID, str] = {}\\n205\\t    used = 0\\n206\\t\\n207\\t    for voucher in result.vouchers:\\n208\\t        is_sales = _is_sales(voucher.voucher_type)\\n209\\t        if not is_sales and not _is_purchase(voucher.voucher_type):\\n210\\t            continue\\n211\\t        pair = _party_and_head(voucher, index, is_sales=is_sales)\\n212\\t        if pair is None:\\n213\\t            continue\\n214\\t        party, head = pair\\n215\\t        observed[(party.id, \\\"SALES\\\" if is_sales else \\\"PURCHASE\\\")][head.id] += 1\\n216\\t        party_names[party.id] = party.name\\n217\\t        used += 1\\n218\\t\\n219\\t    created = updated = skipped = preserved = 0\\n220\\t    now = datetime.now(tz=timezone.utc)\\n221\\t\\n222\\t    for (party_id, kind), counts in observed.items():\\n223\\t        total = sum(counts.values())\\n224\\t        head_id, hits = counts.most_common(1)[0]\\n225\\t        if total < _MIN_VOUCHERS or hits / total < _MIN_MAJORITY:\\n226\\t            skipped += 1\\n227\\t            continue\\n228\\t\\n229\\t        party_name = party_names[party_id]\\n230\\t        vendor = _vendor_for_party(db, company_id, party_name)\\n231\\t        head = db.get(Ledger, head_id)\\n232\\t        if head is None:\\n233\\t            skipped += 1\\n234\\t            continue\\n235\\t\\n236\\t        reason = (\\n237\\t            f\\\"Tally history: {hits} of {total} {kind.lower()} voucher(s) for \\\"\\n238\\t            f\\\"'{party_name}' used '{head.name}'.\\\"\\n239\\t        )\\n240\\t        # Match on either identity a mapping may carry: a manual row created\\n241\\t        # through the API often has only vendor_id, this service's own rows\\n242\\t        # carry the party name. Missing one would add a second active mapping\\n243\\t        # for the same party and make the suggestion AMBIGUOUS.\\n244\\t        identity = [AccountingMapping.counterparty_name == party_name]\\n245\\t        if vendor is not None:\\n246\\t            identity.append(AccountingMapping.vendor_id == vendor.id)\\n247\\t        existing = db.scalar(\\n248\\t            select(AccountingMapping).where(\\n249\\t                AccountingMapping.company_id == company_id,\\n250\\t                AccountingMapping.source_type\\n251\\t                == AccountingMappingSourceType.INVOICE,\\n252\\t                AccountingMapping.voucher_type == kind,\\n253\\t                or_(*identity),\\n254\\t            )\\n255\\t        )\\n256\\t        if existing is not None and existing.transaction_context != _TALLY_PROVENANCE:\\n257\\t            # A person confirmed this one. Tally history never overrides it.\\n258\\t            preserved += 1\\n259\\t            continue\\n260\\t        if existing is None:\\n261\\t            db.add(\\n262\\t                AccountingMapping(\\n263\\t                    company_id=company_id,\\n264\\t                    source_type=AccountingMappingSourceType.INVOICE,\\n265\\t                    match_type=(\\n266\\t                        AccountingMappingMatchType.VENDOR\\n267\\t                        if vendor is not None\\n268\\t                        else AccountingMappingMatchType.COUNTERPARTY\\n269\\t                    ),\\n270\\t                    vendor_id=vendor.id if vendor is not None else None,\\n271\\t                    counterparty_name=party_name,\\n272\\t                    transaction_context=_TALLY_PROVENANCE,\\n273\\t                    voucher_type=kind,\\n274\\t                    ledger_id=head.id,\\n275\\t                    reason=reason,\\n276\\t                    approved_by=actor,\\n277\\t                    approved_at=now,\\n278\\t                    is_active=True,\\n279\\t                )\\n280\\t            )\\n281\\t            created += 1\\n282\\t        elif existing.ledger_id != head.id or existing.reason != reason:\\n283\\t            existing.ledger_id = head.id\\n284\\t            existing.reason = reason\\n285\\t            existing.vendor_id = vendor.id if vendor is not None else None\\n286\\t            existing.match_type = (\\n287\\t                AccountingMappingMatchType.VENDOR\\n288\\t                if vendor is not None\\n289\\t                else AccountingMappingMatchType.COUNTERPARTY\\n290\\t            )\\n291\\t            existing.is_active = True\\n292\\t            existing.approved_by = actor\\n293\\t            existing.approved_at = now\\n294\\t            updated += 1\\n295\\t\\n296\\t    db.commit()\\n297\\t    return TallyMappingSyncResult(\\n298\\t        success=True,\\n299\\t        company_id=company_id,\\n300\\t        tally_company_name=tally_company_name,\\n301\\t        vouchers_fetched=len(result.vouchers),\\n302\\t        vouchers_used=used,\\n303\\t        parties_seen=len(observed),\\n304\\t        mappings_created=created,\\n305\\t        mappings_updated=updated,\\n306\\t        mappings_skipped=skipped,\\n307\\t        mappings_preserved=preserved,\\n308\\t        error_message=None,\\n309\\t    )\\n310\\t\\n311\\t\\n312\\t__all__ = [\\\"TallyMappingSyncResult\\\", {"content":"  1\t\"\"\"Company-scoped Ledger Master.\n  2\t\n  3\tThe client's Phase 1 process starts with a ledger master before draft vouchers\n  4\tcan be prepared. A ledger is deliberately company-owned: the same ledger name\n  5\tmay exist in two companies' charts of accounts, and neither company may use the\n  6\tother's record.\n  7\t\n  8\t`ledger_type` and `parent_group` are free text rather than an invented enum.\n  9\tThe client has not supplied a fixed chart-of-accounts taxonomy, so PAL stores\n 10\tthe company's own grouping instead of guessing accounting categories.\n 11\t\"\"\"\n 12\t\n 13\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n 14\t\n 15\timport enum\n 16\timport uuid\n 17\tfrom datetime import datetime\n 18\t\n 19\tfrom sqlalchemy import (\n 20\t    Boolean,\n 21\t    DateTime,\n 22\t    Enum as SAEnum,\n 23\t    ForeignKey,\n 24\t    String,\n 25\t    UniqueConstraint,\n 26\t    func,\n 27\t)\n 28\tfrom sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID\n 29\tfrom sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column\n 30\t\n 31\tfrom app.core.database import Base\n 32\t\n 33\t\n 34\tclass LedgerSource(str, enum.Enum):\n 35\t    \"\"\"Where the ledger master record came from.\n 36\t\n 37\t    Only `MANUAL` is created by the Phase 1 UI. The other values reserve the\n 38\t    provenance field for the later Tally/Zoho import adapters.\n 39\t    \"\"\"\n 40\t\n 41\t    MANUAL = \"MANUAL\"\n 42\t    IMPORT = \"IMPORT\"\n 43\t    TALLY = \"TALLY\"\n 44\t    ZOHO = \"ZOHO\"\n 45\t\n 46\t\n 47\tLEDGER_SOURCE_ENUM = SAEnum(\n 48\t    LedgerSource,\n 49\t    name=\"ledger_source\",\n 50\t    values_callable=lambda enum_cls: [member.value for member in enum_cls],\n 51\t)\n 52\t\n 53\t\n 54\tclass Ledger(Base):\n 55\t    \"\"\"A ledger in one company's chart of accounts.\"\"\"\n 56\t\n 57\t    __tablename__ = \"ledgers\"\n 58\t    __table_args__ = (\n 59\t        UniqueConstraint(\"company_id\", \"name\", name=\"uq_ledgers_company_name\"),\n 60\t    )\n 61\t\n 62\t    id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(\n 63\t        UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4\n 64\t    )\n 65\t    company_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(\n 66\t        UUID(as_uuid=True),\n 67\t        ForeignKey(\"companies.id\", ondelete=\"RESTRICT\"),\n 68\t        nullable=False,\n 69\t        index=True,\n 70\t    )\n 71\t    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=False)\n 72\t    code: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(64))\n 73\t    #: Company-supplied grouping/category. Not constrained to a PAL-invented list.\n 74\t    ledger_type: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(64))\n 75\t    #: Tally-style parent group, when the company supplies one.\n 76\t    parent_group: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(255))\n 77\t    #: Optional explicit tax classification, e.g. CGST/SGST/IGST/Cess.\n 78\t    tax_classification: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(64))\n 79\t    source: Mapped[LedgerSource] = mapped_column(\n 80\t        LEDGER_SOURCE_ENUM,\n 81\t        nullable=False,\n 82\t        default=LedgerSource.MANUAL,\n 83\t        server_default=LedgerSource.MANUAL.value,\n 84\t    )\n 85\t    external_tally_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(255))\n 86\t    external_zoho_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(255))\n 87\t    is_active: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(\n 88\t        Boolean, nullable=False, default=True, server_default=\"true\"\n 89\t    )\n 90\t    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(\n 91\t        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False, server_default=func.now()\n 92\t    )\n 93\t    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(\n 94\t        DateTime(timezone=True),\n 95\t        nullable=False,\n 96\t        server_default=func.now(),\n 97\t        onupdate=func.now(),\n 98\t    )\n 99\t\n100\t    def __repr__(self) -> str:\n101\t        return (\n102\t            f\"<Ledger id={self.id} company_id={self.company_id} \"\n103\t            f\"name={self.name!r} active={self.is_active}>\"\n104\t        )\n105\t\n106\t\n107\t__all__ = [\"Ledger\", \"LedgerSource\", \"LEDGER_SOURCE_ENUM\"]","bytes":3418,"startLine":1,"endLine":107,"totalLines":107}

The other values reserve the\\n 38\\t    provenance field for the later Tally/Zoho import adapters.\\n 39\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n 40\\t\\n 41\\t    MANUAL = \\\"MANUAL\\\"\\n 42\\t    IMPORT = \\\"IMPORT\\\"\\n 43\\t    TALLY = \\\"TALLY\\\"\\n 44\\t    ZOHO = \\\"ZOHO\\\"\\n 45\\t\\n 46\\t\\n 47\\tLEDGER_SOURCE_ENUM = SAEnum(\\n 48\\t    LedgerSource,\\n 49\\t    name=\\\"ledger_source\\\",\\n 50\\t    values_callable=lambda enum_cls: [member.value for member in enum_cls],\\n 51\\t)\\n 52\\t\\n 53\\t\\n 54\\tclass Ledger(Base):\\n 55\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"A ledger in one company's chart of accounts.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n 56\\t\\n 57\\t    __tablename__ = \\\"ledgers\\\"\\n 58\\t    __table_args__ = (\\n 59\\t        UniqueConstraint(\\\"company_id\\\", \\\"name\\\", name=\\\"uq_ledgers_company_name\\\"),\\n 60\\t    )\\n 61\\t\\n 62\\t    id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(\\n 63\\t        UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4\\n 64\\t    )\\n 65\\t    company_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(\\n 66\\t        UUID(as_uuid=True),\\n 67\\t        ForeignKey(\\\"companies.id\\\", ondelete=\\\"RESTRICT\\\"),\\n 68\\t        nullable=False,\\n 69\\t        index=True,\\n 70\\t    )\\n 71\\t    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=False)\\n 72\\t    code: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(64))\\n 73\\t    #: Company-supplied grouping/category. Not constrained to a PAL-invented list.\\n 74\\t    ledger_type: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(64))\\n 75\\t    #: Tally-style parent group, when the company supplies one.\\n 76\\t    parent_group: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(255))\\n 77\\t    #: Optional explicit tax classification, e.g. CGST/SGST/IGST/Cess.\\n 78\\t    tax_classification: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(64))\\n 79\\t    source: Mapped[LedgerSource] = mapped_column(\\n 80\\t        LEDGER_SOURCE_ENUM,\\n 81\\t        nullable=False,\\n 82\\t        default=LedgerSource.MANUAL,\\n 83\\t        server_default=LedgerSource.MANUAL.value,\\n 84\\t    )\\n 85\\t    external_tally_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(255))\\n 86\\t    external_zoho_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(255))\\n 87\\t    is_active: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(\\n 88\\t        Boolean, nullable=False, default=True, server_default=\\\"true\\\"\\n 89\\t    )\\n 90\\t    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(\\n 91\\t        DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False, server_default=func.now()\\n 92\\t    )\\n 93\\t    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(\\n 94\\t        DateTime(timezone=True),\\n 95\\t        nullable=False,\\n 96\\t        server_default=func.now(),\\n 97\\t        onupdate=func.now(),\\n 98\\t    )\\n 99\\t\\n100\\t    def __repr__(self) -> str:\\n101\\t        return (\\n102\\t            f\\\"<Ledger id={self.id} company_id={self.company_id} \\\"\\n103\\t            f\\\"name={self.name!r} active={self.is_active}>\\\"\\n104\\t        )\\n105\\t\\n106\\t\\n107\\t__all__ = [\\\"Ledger\\\", \\\"LedgerSource\\\", \\\"LEDGER_SOURCE_ENUM\\\"]\",\"bytes\":3418,\"startLine\":1,\"endLine\":107,\"totalLines\":107}\n[trace revision=19 evidence=E0016]\n[trace writeRevision=8]"} -->
