The service does a case-insensitive\n  9\tlookup before creating, so repeated syncs produce no duplicates.\n 10\t\"\"\"\n 11\t\n 12\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n 13\t\n 14\timport uuid\n 15\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n 16\tfrom datetime import date, datetime, timezone\n 17\tfrom decimal import Decimal\n 18\t\n 19\tfrom sqlalchemy import func, select\n 20\tfrom sqlalchemy.orm import Session\n 21\t\n 22\tfrom app.integrations.tally import TallyError, TallyService, get_tally_service\n 23\tfrom app.models.bank import (\n 24\t    BankAccount,\n 25\t    BankStatement,\n 26\t    BankStatementStatus,\n 27\t    BankTransaction,\n 28\t)\n 29\tfrom app.models.company import Company, CompanyStatus\n 30\tfrom app.models.ledger import Ledger, LedgerSource\n 31\tfrom app.models.tally_sync import TallySyncLog\n 32\tfrom app.models.vendor import Vendor\n 33\tfrom app.services.bank_validation import derive_transaction_type\n 34\tfrom app.services.company import CompanyNotFoundError, get_company\n 35\tfrom app.services.ledger import find_ledger_by_name, match_ledger_by_name\n 36\t\n 37\tZERO = Decimal(\"0\")\n 38\t\n 39\t\n 40\tclass TallySyncError(Exception):\n 41\t    \"\"\"Tally connectivity or parsing failure during sync.\"\"\"\n 42\t\n 43\t\n 44\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n 45\tclass TallyCompanySyncResult:\n 46\t    \"\"\"Outcome of one Tally company-master sync run.\"\"\"\n 47\t\n 48\t    success: bool\n 49\t    companies_fetched: int\n 50\t    companies_created: int\n 51\t    companies_updated: int\n 52\t    companies_unchanged: int\n 53\t    error_message: str | None = None\n 54\t\n 55\t\n 56\tdef sync_tally_companies(\n 57\t    db: Session,\n 58\t    *,\n 59\t    service: TallyService | None = None,\n 60\t) -> TallyCompanySyncResult:\n 61\t    \"\"\"Upsert TallyPrime companies into PAL's company master.\n 62\t\n 63\t    Tally is the source for company identity: a company loaded in Tally is\n 64\t    created in PAL with the same name. Existing PAL companies are matched\n 65\t    case-insensitively and are never hard-deleted. If a company was archived\n 66\t    locally (``INACTIVE``) but is still present in Tally, the sync restores it\n 67\t    to ``ACTIVE``. A blank Tally company list is a successful no-op; a Tally\n 68\t    failure is returned as ``success=False`` so the UI can show the error\n 69\t    without treating it as a server crash.\n 70\t    \"\"\"\n 71\t    fetched = created = updated = unchanged = 0\n 72\t    error_message: str | None = None\n 73\t    owned_service = service is None\n 74\t    svc: TallyService | None = service\n 75\t\n 76\t    try:\n 77\t        if svc is None:\n 78\t            svc = get_tally_service()\n 79\t        tally_companies = svc.get_companies()\n 80\t        fetched = len(tally_companies)\n 81\t\n 82\t        for tally_company in tally_companies:\n 83\t            name = (tally_company.name or \"\").strip()\n 84\t            if not name:\n 85\t                continue\n 86\t\n 87\t            existing = db.scalar(\n 88\t                select(Company).where(func.lower(Company.name) == name.lower())\n 89\t            )\n 90\t            if existing is None:\n 91\t                db.add(\n 92\t                    Company(\n 93\t                        name=name,\n 94\t                        legal_name=name,\n 95\t                        status=CompanyStatus.ACTIVE,\n 96\t                    )\n 97\t                )\n 98\t                created += 1\n 99\t                continue\n100\t\n101\t            changed = False\n102\t            if existing.name != name:\n103\t                existing.name = name\n104\t                changed = True\n105\t            if not (existing.legal_name or \"\").strip():\n106\t                existing.legal_name = name\n107\t                changed = True\n108\t            if existing.status == CompanyStatus.INACTIVE:\n109\t                existing.status = CompanyStatus.ACTIVE\n110\t                changed = True\n111\t\n112\t            if changed:\n113\t                updated += 1\n114\t            else:\n115\t                unchanged += 1\n116\t\n117\t        db.commit()\n118\t    except TallyError as exc:\n119\t        db.rollback()\n120\t        error_message = str(exc)\n121\t    except Exception:\n122\t        db.rollback()\n123\t        raise\n124\t    finally:\n125\t        if owned_service and svc is not None:\n126\t            svc.close()\n127\t\n128\t    return TallyCompanySyncResult(\n129\t        success=error_message is None,\n130\t        companies_fetched=fetched,\n131\t        companies_created=created,\n132\t        companies_updated=updated,\n133\t        companies_unchanged=unchanged,\n134\t        error_message=error_message,\n135\t    )\n136\t\n137\t\n138\tdef _upsert_ledgers(\n139\t    db: Session,\n140\t    company_id: uuid.UUID,\n141\t    tally_ledgers,\n142\t) -> tuple[int, int, int]:\n143\t    \"\"\"Upsert Tally ledgers into PAL. Returns (created, updated, unchanged).\"\"\"\n144\t    created = updated = unchanged = 0\n145\t\n146\t    for tl in tally_ledgers:\n147\t        name = (tl.name or \"\").strip()\n148\t        if not name:\n149\t            continue\n150\t\n151\t        existing = db.scalar(\n152\t            select(Ledger).where(\n153\t                Ledger.company_id == company_id,\n154\t                func.lower(Ledger.name) == name.lower(),\n155\t            )\n156\t        )\n157\t\n158\t        if existing is None:\n159\t            ledger = Ledger(\n160\t                company_id=company_id,\n161\t                name=name,\n162\t                parent_group=tl.parent,\n163\t                source=LedgerSource.TALLY,\n164\t                external_tally_id=name,\n165\t                is_active=True,\n166\t            )\n167\t            db.add(ledger)\n168\t            created += 1\n169\t        else:\n170\t            new_parent = tl.parent or None\n171\t            if existing.parent_group != new_parent:\n172\t                existing.parent_group = new_parent\n173\t                updated += 1\n174\t            else:\n175\t                unchanged += 1\n176\t\n177\t    return created, updated, unchanged\n178\t\n179\t\n180\t#: Preferred Tally names for each company-level ledger field, best first. Only\n181\t#: an exact (case-insensitive) hit counts, so an unusual chart of accounts\n182\t#: leaves the field unset for a person to pick rather than guessing between\n183\t#: lookalikes such as \"CGST Input\" and \"Output CGST_KA\".\n184\t_COMPANY_LEDGER_PREFERENCES: tuple[tuple[str, tuple[str, ...]], ...] = (\n185\t    (\"cgst_ledger\", (\"CGST Input\", \"Input CGST\", \"CGST\")),\n186\t    (\"sgst_ledger\", (\"SGST Input\", \"Input SGST\", \"SGST\")),\n187\t    (\"igst_ledger\", (\"IGST Input\", \"Input IGST\", \"IGST\")),\n188\t    (\"cess_ledger\", (\"Cess Input\", \"Input Cess\", \"Cess\")),\n189\t    (\"round_off_ledger\", (\"Round Off\", \"Rounded Off\", \"Round-off\")),\n190\t    (\"sales_ledger\", (\"Sales\", \"Sales Accounts\", \"Sales A/c\")),\n191\t)\n192\t\n193\t#: Tally group holding party/creditor ledgers.\n194\t_CREDITOR_GROUP = \"Sundry Creditors\"\n195\t\n196\t\n197\tdef _automap_company_ledgers(db: Session, company_id: uuid.UUID) -> int:\n198\t    \"\"\"Fill unset company ledger fields from the synced ledger master.\"\"\"\n199\t    company = get_company(db, company_id)\n200\t    filled = 0\n201\t    for field_name, preferences in _COMPANY_LEDGER_PREFERENCES:\n202\t        if (getattr(company, field_name) or \"\").strip():\n203\t            continue\n204\t        for preference in preferences:\n205\t            ledger = find_ledger_by_name(db, company_id, preference)\n206\t            if ledger is not None:\n207\t                setattr(company, field_name, ledger.name)\n208\t                filled += 1\n209\t                break\n210\t    return filled\n211\t\n212\t\n213\tdef _automap_vendor_ledgers(db: Session, company_id: uuid.UUID) -> int:\n214\t    \"\"\"Point each vendor's creditor ledger at a real Tally ledger.\n215\t\n216\t    A vendor auto-created from an invoice carries the printed party name as its\n217\t    creditor ledger, which rarely matches Tally's own spelling (\"RADHIKA DEVCON\n218\t    PRIVATE LIMITED\" against \"RADHIKA DWCON PVT LTD\"). Only names that do not\n219\t    already resolve are touched, so a reviewer's correction is never undone.\n220\t    \"\"\"\n221\t    vendors = list(\n222\t        db.scalars(select(Vendor).where(Vendor.company_id == company_id)).all()\n223\t    )\n224\t    remapped = 0\n225\t    for vendor in vendors:\n226\t        current = (vendor.creditor_ledger or \"\").strip()\n227\t        if current and find_ledger_by_name(db, company_id, current) is not None:\n228\t            continue\n229\t        ledger = match_ledger_by_name(\n230\t            db,\n231\t            company_id,\n232\t            current or vendor.name,\n233\t            parent_group=_CREDITOR_GROUP,\n234\t        )\n235\t        if ledger is not None and ledger.name != current:\n236\t            vendor.creditor_ledger = ledger.name\n237\t            remapped += 1\n238\t    return remapped\n239\t\n240\t\n241\tdef sync_tally_ledgers(\n242\t    db: Session,\n243\t    company_id: uuid.UUID,\n244\t    tally_company_name: str,\n245\t    *,\n246\t    actor: str = \"local-user\",\n247\t    service: TallyService | None = None,\n248\t) -> TallySyncLog:\n249\t    \"\"\"Fetch ledgers from TallyPrime and upsert into the PAL ledger master.\n250\t\n251\t    Once the master is up to date, unset company tax/sales ledgers and\n252\t    unresolved vendor creditor ledgers are pointed at the synced ledgers so\n253\t    vouchers stop reporting MISSING_LEDGER for ledgers Tally already has. Only\n254\t    blank or unresolvable values are filled; a reviewer's own choice stands.\n255\t\n256\t    Returns a TallySyncLog record. On Tally failure the log records the error\n257\t    and no ledger rows are committed.\n258\t\n259\t    Raises CompanyNotFoundError if company_id does not exist in PAL.\n260\t    \"\"\"\n261\t    get_company(db, company_id)\n262\t\n263\t    tally_company_name = tally_company_name.strip()\n264\t    owned_service = service is None\n265\t    svc: TallyService = service or get_tally_service()\n266\t\n267\t    error_message: str | None = None\n268\t    fetched = created = updated = unchanged = 0\n269\t\n270\t    try:\n271\t        result = svc.get_ledgers(tally_company_name)\n272\t        fetched = len(result.ledgers)\n273\t        created, updated, unchanged = _upsert_ledgers(db, company_id, result.ledgers)\n274\t        # Flush so the auto-map below resolves names against this run's rows.\n275\t        db.flush()\n276\t        _automap_company_ledgers(db, company_id)\n277\t        _automap_vendor_ledgers(db, company_id)\n278\t    except TallyError as exc:\n279\t        error_message = str(exc)\n280\t    finally:\n281\t        if owned_service:\n282\t            svc.close()\n283\t\n284\t    log = TallySyncLog(\n285\t        company_id=company_id,\n286\t        tally_company_name=tally_company_name,\n287\t        synced_at=datetime.now(tz=timezone.utc),\n288\t        ledgers_fetched=fetched,\n289\t        ledgers_created=created,\n290\t        ledgers_updated=updated,\n291\t        ledgers_unchanged=unchanged,\n292\t        error_message=error_message,\n293\t        triggered_by=actor,\n294\t    )\n295\t    db.add(log)\n296\t    db.commit()\n297\t    db.refresh(log)\n298\t    return log\n299\t\n300\t\n301\tdef get_latest_sync(\n302\t    db: Session,\n303\t    company_id: uuid.UUID,\n304\t) -> TallySyncLog | None:\n305\t    \"\"\"Return the most recent sync log for this company, or None.\"\"\"\n306\t    return db.scalar(\n307\t        select(TallySyncLog)\n308\t        .where(TallySyncLog.company_id == company_id)\n309\t        .order_by(TallySyncLog.synced_at.desc())\n310\t        .limit(1)\n311\t    )\n312\t\n313\t\n314\t# Tally groups that represent bank accounts.\n315\t_BANK_GROUPS = {\"bank accounts\", \"bank od account\", \"bank od accounts\"}\n316\t\n317\t#: Groups where a Tally debit increases the bank balance. For an overdraft or\n318\t#: credit-card ledger the direction is the other way round.\n319\t_DEPOSIT_GROUPS = {\"bank accounts\"}\n320\t\n321\t# Placeholder stored when Tally has no account number for an imported account.\n322\t_TALLY_ACCOUNT_NUMBER = \"TALLY-IMPORT\"\n323\t\n324\t#: Marks a statement PAL created from a Tally voucher read, so the next sync\n325\t#: finds the same statement instead of appending to an uploaded one.\n326\t_TALLY_STATEMENT_SOURCE = \"TALLY\"\n327\t\n328\t\n329\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n330\tclass TallyBankSyncResult:\n331\t    \"\"\"Outcome of one Tally bank-account sync run.\"\"\"\n332\t\n333\t    success: bool\n334\t    tally_company_name: str\n335\t    accounts_fetched: int\n336\t    accounts_created: int\n337\t    accounts_updated: int\n338\t    accounts_unchanged: int\n339\t    error_message: str | None = None\n340\t\n341\t\n342\tdef _bank_ledgers(ledgers) -> list:\n343\t    \"\"\"Return the ledgers Tally groups as bank accounts.\"\"\"\n344\t    return [\n345\t        ledger\n346\t        for ledger in ledgers\n347\t        if (ledger.parent or \"\").strip().lower() in _BANK_GROUPS\n348\t    ]\n349\t\n350\t\n351\tdef _upsert_bank_accounts(\n352\t    db: Session,\n353\t    company_id: uuid.UUID,\n354\t    bank_ledgers,\n355\t) -> tuple[int, int, int]:\n356\t    \"\"\"Upsert Tally bank ledgers into PAL accounts.\n357\t\n358\t    Returns (created, updated, unchanged). Matching is by ledger_name,\n359\t    case-insensitively, so repeated syncs never duplicate an account.\n360\t    \"\"\"\n361\t    created = updated = unchanged = 0\n362\t\n363\t    for ledger in bank_ledgers:\n364\t        name = (ledger.name or \"\").strip()\n365\t        if not name:\n366\t            continue\n367\t\n368\t        existing = db.scalar(\n369\t            select(BankAccount).where(\n370\t                BankAccount.company_id == company_id,\n371\t                func.lower(BankAccount.ledger_name) == name.lower(),\n372\t            )\n373\t derive_transaction_type\\n 34\\tfrom app.services.company import CompanyNotFoundError, get_company\\n 35\\tfrom app.services.ledger import find_ledger_by_name, match_ledger_by_name\\n 36\\t\\n 37\\tZERO = Decimal(\\\"0\\\")\\n 38\\t\\n 39\\t\\n 40\\tclass TallySyncError(Exception):\\n 41\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"Tally connectivity or parsing failure during sync.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n 42\\t\\n 43\\t\\n 44\\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\\n 45\\tclass TallyCompanySyncResult:\\n 46\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"Outcome of one Tally company-master sync run.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n 47\\t\\n 48\\t    success: bool\\n 49\\t    companies_fetched: int\\n 50\\t    companies_created: int\\n 51\\t    companies_updated: int\\n 52\\t    companies_unchanged: int\\n 53\\t    error_message: str | None = None\\n 54\\t\\n 55\\t\\n 56\\tdef sync_tally_companies(\\n 57\\t    db: Session,\\n 58\\t    *,\\n 59\\t    service: TallyService | None = None,\\n 60\\t) -> TallyCompanySyncResult:\\n 61\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"Upsert TallyPrime companies into PAL's company master.\\n 62\\t\\n 63\\t    Tally is the source for company identity: a company loaded in Tally is\\n 64\\t    created in PAL with the same name. Existing PAL companies are matched\\n 65\\t    case-insensitively and are never hard-deleted. If a company was archived\\n 66\\t    locally (``INACTIVE``) but is still present in Tally, the sync restores it\\n 67\\t    to ``ACTIVE``. A blank Tally company list is a successful no-op; a Tally\\n 68\\t    failure is returned as ``success=False`` so the UI can show the error\\n 69\\t    without treating it as a server crash.\\n 70\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n 71\\t    fetched = created = updated = unchanged = 0\\n 72\\t    error_message: str | None = None\\n 73\\t    owned_service = service is None\\n 74\\t    svc: TallyService | None = service\\n 75\\t\\n 76\\t    try:\\n 77\\t        if svc is None:\\n 78\\t            svc = get_tally_service()\\n 79\\t        tally_companies = svc.get_companies()\\n 80\\t        fetched = len(tally_companies)\\n 81\\t\\n 82\\t        for tally_company in tally_companies:\\n 83\\t            name = (tally_company.name or \\\"\\\").strip()\\n 84\\t            if not name:\\n 85\\t                continue\\n 86\\t\\n 87\\t            existing = db.scalar(\\n 88\\t                select(Company).where(func.lower(Company.name) == name.lower())\\n 89\\t            )\\n 90\\t            if existing is None:\\n 91\\t                db.add(\\n 92\\t                    Company(\\n 93\\t                        name=name,\\n 94\\t                        legal_name=name,\\n 95\\t                        status=CompanyStatus.ACTIVE,\\n 96\\t                    )\\n 97\\t                )\\n 98\\t                created += 1\\n 99\\t                continue\\n100\\t\\n101\\t            changed = False\\n102\\t            if existing.name != name:\\n103\\t                existing.name = name\\n104\\t                changed = True\\n105\\t            if not (existing.legal_name or \\\"\\\").strip():\\n106\\t                existing.legal_name = name\\n107\\t                changed = True\\n108\\t            if existing.status == CompanyStatus.INACTIVE:\\n109\\t                existing.status = CompanyStatus.ACTIVE\\n110\\t                changed = True\\n111\\t\\n112\\t            if changed:\\n113\\t                updated += 1\\n114\\t            else:\\n115\\t                unchanged += 1\\n116\\t\\n117\\t        db.commit()\\n118\\t    except TallyError as exc:\\n119\\t        db.rollback()\\n120\\t        error_message = str(exc)\\n121\\t    except Exception:\\n122\\t        db.rollback()\\n123\\t        raise\\n124\\t    finally:\\n125\\t        if owned_service and svc is not None:\\n126\\t            svc.close()\\n127\\t\\n128\\t    return TallyCompanySyncResult(\\n129\\t        success=error_message is None,\\n130\\t        companies_fetched=fetched,\\n131\\t        companies_created=created,\\n132\\t        companies_updated=updated,\\n133\\t        companies_unchanged=unchanged,\\n134\\t        error_message=error_message,\\n135\\t    )\\n136\\t\\n137\\t\\n138\\tdef _upsert_ledgers(\\n139\\t    db: Session,\\n140\\t    company_id: uuid.UUID,\\n141\\t    tally_ledgers,\\n142\\t) -> tuple[int, int, int]:\\n143\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"Upsert Tally ledgers into PAL. Returns (created, updated, unchanged).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n144\\t    created = updated = unchanged = 0\\n145\\t\\n146\\t    for tl in tally_ledgers:\\n147\\t        name = (tl.name or \\\"\\\").strip()\\n148\\t        if not name:\\n149\\t            continue\\n150\\t\\n151\\t        existing = db.scalar(\\n152\\t            select(Ledger).where(\\n153\\t                Ledger.company_id == company_id,\\n154\\t                func.lower(Ledger.name) == name.lower(),\\n155\\t            )\\n156\\t        )\\n157\\t\\n158\\t        if existing is None:\\n159\\t            ledger = Ledger(\\n160\\t                company_id=company_id,\\n161\\t                name=name,\\n162\\t                parent_group=tl.parent,\\n163\\t                source=LedgerSource.TALLY,\\n164\\t                external_tally_id=name,\\n165\\t                is_active=True,\\n166\\t            )\\n167\\t            db.add(ledger)\\n168\\t            created += 1\\n169\\t        else:\\n170\\t            new_parent = tl.parent or None\\n171\\t            if existing.parent_group != new_parent:\\n172\\t                existing.parent_group = new_parent\\n173\\t                updated += 1\\n174\\t            else:\\n175\\t                unchanged += 1\\n176\\t\\n177\\t    return created, updated, unchanged\\n178\\t\\n179\\t\\n180\\t#: Preferred Tally names for each company-level ledger field, best first. Only\\n181\\t#: an exact (case-insensitive) hit counts, so an unusual chart of accounts\\n182\\t#: leaves the field unset for a person to pick rather than guessing between\\n183\\t#: lookalikes such as \\\"CGST Input\\\" and \\\"Output CGST_KA\\\".\\n184\\t_COMPANY_LEDGER_PREFERENCES: tuple[tuple[str, tuple[str, ...]], ...] = (\\n185\\t    (\\\"cgst_ledger\\\", (\\\"CGST Input\\\", \\\"Input CGST\\\", \\\"CGST\\\")),\\n186\\t    (\\\"sgst_ledger\\\", (\\\"SGST Input\\\", \\\"Input SGST\\\", \\\"SGST\\\")),\\n187\\t    (\\\"igst_ledger\\\", (\\\"IGST Input\\\", \\\"Input IGST\\\", \\\"IGST\\\")),\\n188\\t    (\\\"cess_ledger\\\", (\\\"Cess Input\\\", \\\"Input Cess\\\", \\\"Cess\\\")),\\n189\\t    (\\\"round_off_ledger\\\", (\\\"Round Off\\\", \\\"Rounded Off\\\", \\\"Round-off\\\")),\\n190\\t    (\\\"sales_ledger\\\", (\\\"Sales\\\", \\\"Sales Accounts\\\", \\\"Sales A/c\\\")),\\n191\\t)\\n192\\t\\n193\\t#: Tally group holding party/creditor ledgers.\\n194\\t_CREDITOR_GROUP = \\\"Sundry Creditors\\\"\\n195\\t\\n196\\t\\n197\\tdef _automap_company_ledgers(db: Session, company_id: uuid.UUID) -> int:\\n198\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"Fill unset company ledger fields from the synced ledger master.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n199\\t    company = get_company(db, company_id)\\n200\\t    filled = 0\\n201\\t    for field_name, preferences in _COMPANY_LEDGER_PREFERENCES:\\n202\\t        if (getattr(company, field_name) or \\\"\\\").strip():\\n203\\t            continue\\n204\\t        for preference in preferences:\\n205\\t            ledger = find_ledger_by_name(db, company_id, preference)\\n206\\t            if ledger is not None:\\n207\\t                setattr(company, field_name, ledger.name)\\n208\\t                filled += 1\\n209\\t                break\\n210\\t    return filled\\n211\\t\\n212\\t\\n213\\tdef _automap_vendor_ledgers(db: Session, company_id: uuid.UUID) -> int:\\n214\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"Point each vendor's creditor ledger at a real Tally ledger.\\n215\\t\\n216\\t    A vendor auto-created from an invoice carries the printed party name as its\\n217\\t    creditor ledger, which rarely matches Tally's own spelling (\\\"RADHIKA DEVCON\\n218\\t    PRIVATE LIMITED\\\" against \\\"RADHIKA DWCON PVT LTD\\\"). Only names that do not\\n219\\t    already resolve are touched, so a reviewer's correction is never undone.\\n220\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n221\\t    vendors = list(\\n222\\t        db.scalars(select(Vendor).where(Vendor.company_id == company_id)).all()\\n223\\t    )\\n224\\t    remapped = 0\\n225\\t    for vendor in vendors:\\n226\\t        current = (vendor.creditor_ledger or \\\"\\\").strip()\\n227\\t        if current and find_ledger_by_name(db, company_id, current) is not None:\\n228\\t            continue\\n229\\t        ledger = match_ledger_by_name(\\n230\\t            db,\\n231\\t            company_id,\\n232\\t            current or vendor.name,\\n233\\t            parent_group=_CREDITOR_GROUP,\\n234\\t        )\\n235\\t        if ledger is not None and ledger.name != current:\\n236\\t            vendor.creditor_ledger = ledger.name\\n237\\t            remapped += 1\\n238\\t    return remapped\\n239\\t\\n240\\t\\n241\\tdef sync_tally_ledgers(\\n242\\t    db: Session,\\n243\\t    company_id: uuid.UUID,\\n244\\t    tally_company_name: str,\\n245\\t    *,\\n246\\t    actor: str = \\\"local-user\\\",\\n247\\t    service: TallyService | None = None,\\n248\\t) -> TallySyncLog:\\n249\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"Fetch ledgers from TallyPrime and upsert into the PAL ledger master.\\n250\\t\\n251\\t    Once the master is up to date, unset company tax/sales ledgers and\\n252\\t    unresolved vendor creditor ledgers are pointed at the synced ledgers so\\n253\\t    vouchers stop reporting MISSING_LEDGER for ledgers Tally already has. Only\\n254\\t    blank or unresolvable values are filled; a reviewer's own choice stands.\\n255\\t\\n256\\t    Returns a TallySyncLog record. On Tally failure the log records the error\\n257\\t    and no ledger rows are committed.\\n258\\t\\n259\\t    Raises CompanyNotFoundError if company_id does not exist in PAL.\\n260\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n261\\t    get_company(db, company_id)\\n262\\t\\n263\\t    tally_company_name = tally_company_name.strip()\\n264\\t    owned_service = service is None\\n265\\t    svc: TallyService = service or get_tally_service()\\n266\\t\\n267\\t    error_message: str | None = None\\n268\\t    fetched = created = updated = unchanged = 0\\n269\\t\\n270\\t    try:\\n271\\t        result = svc.get_ledgers(tally_company_name)\\n272\\t        fetched = len(result.ledgers)\\n273\\t        created, updated, unchanged = _upsert_ledgers(db, company_id, result.ledgers)\\n274\\t        # Flush so the auto-map below resolves names against this run's rows.\\n275\\t        db.flush()\\n276\\t        _automap_company_ledgers(db, company_id)\\n277\\t        _automap_vendor_ledgers(db, company_id)\\n278\\t    except TallyError as exc:\\n279\\t        error_message = str(exc)\\n280\\t    finally:\\n281\\t        if owned_service:\\n282\\t            svc.close()\\n283\\t\\n284\\t    log = TallySyncLog(\\n285\\t        company_id=company_id,\\n286\\t        tally_company_name=tally_company_name,\\n287\\t        synced_at=datetime.now(tz=timezone.utc),\\n288\\t        ledgers_fetched=fetched,\\n289\\t        ledgers_created=created,\\n290\\t        ledgers_updated=updated,\\n291\\t        ledgers_unchanged=unchanged,\\n292\\t        error_message=error_message,\\n293\\t        triggered_by=actor,\\n294\\t    )\\n295\\t    db.add(log)\\n296\\t    db.commit()\\n297\\t    db.refresh(log)\\n298\\t    return log\\n299\\t\\n300\\t\\n301\\tdef get_latest_sync(\\n302\\t    db: Session,\\n303\\t    company_id: uuid.UUID,\\n304\\t) -> TallySyncLog | None:\\n305\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"Return the most recent sync log for this company, or None.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n306\\t    return db.scalar(\\n307\\t        select(TallySyncLog)\\n308\\t        .where(TallySyncLog.company_id == company_id)\\n309\\t        .order_by(TallySyncLog.synced_at.desc())\\n310\\t        .limit(1)\\n311\\t    )\\n312\\t\\n313\\t\\n314\\t# Tally groups that represent bank accounts.\\n315\\t_BANK_GROUPS = {\\\"bank accounts\\\", \\\"bank od account\\\", \\\"bank od accounts\\\"}\\n316\\t\\n317\\t#: Groups where a Tally debit increases the bank balance. For an overdraft or\\n318\\t#: credit-card ledger the direction is the other way round.\\n319\\t_DEPOSIT_GROUPS = {\\\"bank accounts\\\"}\\n320\\t\\n321\\t# Placeholder stored when Tally has no account number for an imported account.\\n322\\t_TALLY_ACCOUNT_NUMBER = \\\"TALLY-IMPORT\\\"\\n323\\t\\n324\\t#: Marks a statement PAL created from a Tally voucher read, so the next sync\\n325\\t#: finds the same statement instead of appending to an uploaded one.\\n326\\t_TALLY_STATEMENT_SOURCE = \\\"TALLY\\\"\\n327\\t\\n328\\t\\n329\\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\\n330\\tclass TallyBankSyncResult:\\n331\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"Outcome of one Tally bank-account sync run.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n332\\t\\n333\\t    success: bool\\n334\\t    tally_company_name: str\\n335\\t    accounts_fetched: int\\n336\\t    accounts_created: int\\n337\\t    accounts_updated: int\\n338\\t    accounts_unchanged: int\\n339\\t    error_message: str | None = None\\n340\\t\\n341\\t\\n342\\tdef _bank_ledgers(ledgers) -> list:\\n343\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"Return the ledgers Tally groups as bank accounts.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n344\\t    return [\\n345\\t        ledger\\n346\\t        for ledger in ledgers\\n347\\t        if (ledger.parent or \\\"\\\").strip().lower() in _BANK_GROUPS\\n348\\t    ]\\n349\\t\\n350\\t\\n351\\tdef _upsert_bank_accounts(\\n352\\t    db: Session,\\n353\\t    company_id: uuid.UUID,\\n354\\t    bank_ledgers,\\n355\\t) -> tuple[int, int, int]:\\n356\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"Upsert Tally bank ledgers into PAL accounts.\\n357\\t\\n358\\t    Returns (created, updated, unchanged). Matching is by ledger_name,\\n359\\t    case-insensitively, so repeated syncs never duplicate an account.\\n360\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n361\\t    created = updated = unchanged = 0\\n362\\t\\n363\\t    for ledger in bank_ledgers:\\n364\\t        name = (ledger.name or \\\"\\\").strip()\\n365\\t        if not name:\\n366\\t            continue\\n367\\t\\n368\\t        existing = db.scalar(\\n369\\t            select(BankAccount).where(\\n370\\t                BankAccount.company_id == company_id,\\n371\\t                func.lower(BankAccount.ledger_name) == name.lower(),\\n372\\t            )\\n373\\t        )\\n374\\t\\n375\\t        if existing is None:\\n376\\t            db.add(\\n377\\t                BankAccount(\\n378\\t                    company_id=company_id,\\n379\\t                    bank_name=name,\\n380\\t                    account_name=name,\\n381\\t                    account_number=_TALLY_ACCOUNT_NUMBER,\\n382\\t                    ledger_name=name,\\n383\\t                    currency=\\\"INR\\\",\\n384\\t                    is_active=True,\\n385\\t                )\\n386\\t            )\\n387\\t            created += 1\\n388\\t            continue\\n389\\t\\n390\\t        changed = False\\n391\\t        if not existing.is_active:\\n392\\t            existing.is_active = True\\n393\\t            changed = True\\n394\\t        if existing.bank_name != name:\\n395\\t            existing.bank_name = name\\n396\\t            changed = True\\n397\\t        if changed:\\n398\\t            updated += 1\\n399\\t        else:\\n400\\t            unchanged += 1\\n401\\t\\n402\\t    return created, updated, unchanged\\n403\\t\\n404\\t\\n405\\tdef sync_tally_bank_accounts(\\n406\\t    db: Session,\\n407\\t    company_id: uuid.UUID,\\n408\\t    tally_company_name: str,\\n409\\t    *,\\n410\\t    service: TallyService | None = None,\\n411\\t) -> TallyBankSyncResult:\\n412\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"Fetch bank-account ledgers from Tally and upsert into PAL bank_accounts.\\n413\\t\\n414\\t    Tally ledgers whose parent group is \\\"Bank Accounts\\\" or \\\"Bank OD Account\\\"\\n415\\t    are treated as bank accounts. Matching is by ledger_name (case-insensitive).\\n416\\t    The account_number is set to TALLY-IMPORT as a placeholder; update it with\\n417\\t    the real number after importing.\\n418\\t\\n419\\t    Raises CompanyNotFoundError when company_id is not in PAL.\\n420\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n421\\t    get_company(db, company_id)\\n422\\t\\n423\\t    tally_company_name = tally_company_name.strip()\\n424\\t    owned_service = service is None\\n425\\t    svc: TallyService = service or get_tally_service()\\n426\\t\\n427\\t    fetched = created = updated = unchanged = 0\\n428\\t    error_message: str | None = None\\n429\\t\\n430\\t    try:\\n431\\t        result = svc.get_ledgers(tally_company_name)\\n432\\t        bank_ledgers = _bank_ledgers(result.ledgers)\\n433\\t        fetched = len(bank_ledgers)\\n434\\t        created, updated, unchanged = _upsert_bank_accounts(\\n435\\t            db, company_id, bank_ledgers\\n436\\t        )\\n437\\t        db.commit()\\n438\\t    except TallyError as exc:\\n439\\t        db.rollback()\\n440\\t        error_message = str(exc)\\n441\\t    except Exception:\\n\\n… 250 more line(s). {"content":"  1\t\"\"\"TallyPrime connectivity, master-read and sync endpoints.\n  2\t\n  3\tThe Tally URL is read from server-side settings; callers cannot supply one.\n  4\tSync writes only to PAL's ledger master for the specified company; it never\n  5\tmodifies invoices, vouchers, or other accounting records.\n  6\t\"\"\"\n  7\t\n  8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n  9\t\n 10\timport logging\n 11\tfrom typing import NoReturn\n 12\tfrom uuid import UUID\n 13\t\n 14\tfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query, status\n 15\tfrom sqlalchemy.orm import Session\n 16\t\n 17\tfrom app.api.v1.deps import get_actor\n 18\tfrom app.core.config import get_settings\n 19\tfrom app.core.database import get_db\n 20\tfrom app.integrations.tally import (\n 21\t    TallyError,\n 22\t    TallyService,\n 23\t    get_tally_service,\n 24\t    safe_display_url,\n 25\t)\n 26\tfrom app.schemas.tally import (\n 27\t    TallyBankDataSyncRequest,\n 28\t    TallyBankDataSyncResponse,\n 29\t    TallyBankSyncResponse,\n 30\t    TallyCompaniesResponse,\n 31\t    TallyCompanyResponse,\n 32\t    TallyCompanySyncResponse,\n 33\t    TallyLatestSyncResponse,\n 34\t    TallyLedgerResponse,\n 35\t    TallyLedgersResponse,\n 36\t    TallyMappingSyncResponse,\n 37\t    TallyStatusResponse,\n 38\t    TallySyncRequest,\n 39\t    TallySyncResponse,\n 40\t)\n 41\tfrom app.services.company import CompanyNotFoundError\n 42\tfrom app.services.tally_mapping_sync import sync_tally_accounting_mappings\n 43\tfrom app.services.tally_sync import (\n 44\t    get_latest_sync,\n 45\t    sync_tally_bank_accounts,\n 46\t    sync_tally_bank_data,\n 47\t    sync_tally_companies,\n 48\t    sync_tally_ledgers,\n 49\t)\n 50\t\n 51\trouter = APIRouter()\n 52\tlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n 53\t\n 54\t_ERROR_STATUS = {\n 55\t    \"configuration_error\": status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,\n 56\t    \"timeout\": status.HTTP_504_GATEWAY_TIMEOUT,\n 57\t    \"connection_error\": status.HTTP_502_BAD_GATEWAY,\n 58\t    \"http_error\": status.HTTP_502_BAD_GATEWAY,\n 59\t    \"invalid_response\": status.HTTP_502_BAD_GATEWAY,\n 60\t}\n 61\t\n 62\t\n 63\tdef _raise_tally_http(exc: TallyError) -> NoReturn:\n 64\t    \"\"\"Map a safe Tally error to an HTTP response without leaking details.\"\"\"\n 65\t    raise HTTPException(\n 66\t        status_code=_ERROR_STATUS.get(exc.code, status.HTTP_502_BAD_GATEWAY),\n 67\t        detail=str(exc),\n 68\t    ) from None\n 69\t\n 70\t\n 71\t@router.get(\n 72\t    \"/status\",\n 73\t    response_model=TallyStatusResponse,\n 74\t    summary=\"Check TallyPrime connectivity\",\n 75\t)\n 76\tdef tally_status() -> TallyStatusResponse:\n 77\t    \"\"\"Actually POST a company-list request to TallyPrime.\n 78\t\n 79\t    The endpoint always returns HTTP 200: a disconnected Tally is a normal\n 80\t    operational state for PAL, not a server error. The response contains only\n 81\t    the configured URL, a boolean and a safe message.\n 82\t    \"\"\"\n 83\t    settings = get_settings()\n 84\t    service: TallyService | None = None\n 85\t    connected = False\n 86\t    try:\n 87\t        service = get_tally_service()\n 88\t        connected = service.check_connection()\n 89\t    except TallyError as exc:\n 90\t        logger.warning(\"TallyPrime status check failed: %s\", exc)\n 91\t    finally:\n 92\t        if service is not None:\n 93\t            service.close()\n 94\t\n 95\t    return TallyStatusResponse(\n 96\t        connected=connected,\n 97\t        url=safe_display_url(settings.tally_base_url),\n 98\t        message=(\n 99\t            \"TallyPrime is reachable\"\n100\t            if connected\n101\t            else \"Unable to connect to TallyPrime\"\n102\t        ),\n103\t    )\n104\t\n105\t\n106\t@router.get(\n107\t    \"/companies\",\n108\t    response_model=TallyCompaniesResponse,\n109\t    summary=\"Read companies from TallyPrime\",\n110\t)\n111\tdef tally_companies() -> TallyCompaniesResponse:\n112\t    \"\"\"Return every company TallyPrime reports for the company collection.\"\"\"\n113\t    service: TallyService | None = None\n114\t    try:\n115\t        service = get_tally_service()\n116\t        companies = service.get_companies()\n117\t    except TallyError as exc:\n118\t        _raise_tally_http(exc)\n119\t    finally:\n120\t        if service is not None:\n121\t            service.close()\n122\t\n123\t    return TallyCompaniesResponse(\n124\t        success=True,\n125\t        companies=[TallyCompanyResponse(name=company.name) for company in companies],\n126\t        message=None if companies else \"No company is loaded in TallyPrime.\",\n127\t    )\n128\t\n129\t\n130\t@router.post(\n131\t    \"/companies/sync\",\n132\t    response_model=TallyCompanySyncResponse,\n133\t    summary=\"Sync TallyPrime companies into PAL company master\",\n134\t)\n135\tdef tally_sync_companies(\n136\t    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n137\t) -> TallyCompanySyncResponse:\n138\t    \"\"\"Import TallyPrime companies into PAL's company master.\n139\t\n140\t    Tally is treated as the source of company identity. Companies are matched\n141\t    case-insensitively and never deleted. A Tally failure returns HTTP 200 with\n142\t    ``success=False`` so the UI can show the problem without a crash page.\n143\t    \"\"\"\n144\t    result = sync_tally_companies(db)\n145\t    return TallyCompanySyncResponse(\n146\t        success=result.success,\n147\t        companies_fetched=result.companies_fetched,\n148\t        companies_created=result.companies_created,\n149\t        companies_updated=result.companies_updated,\n150\t        companies_unchanged=result.companies_unchanged,\n151\t        error_message=result.error_message,\n152\t    )\n153\t\n154\t\n155\t@router.post(\n156\t    \"/sync/{company_id}\",\n157\t    response_model=TallySyncResponse,\n158\t    summary=\"Sync TallyPrime ledgers into PAL ledger master\",\n159\t)\n160\tdef tally_sync(\n161\t    company_id: UUID,\n162\t    body: TallySyncRequest,\n163\t    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n164\t    actor: str = Depends(get_actor),\n165\t) -> TallySyncResponse:\n166\t    \"\"\"Fetch ledgers from TallyPrime and upsert into this company's ledger master.\n167\t\n168\t    Existing PAL data is never deleted. Repeated calls are idempotent: a ledger\n169\t    already in PAL is updated (if parent changed) or left unchanged, never duplicated.\n170\t    On Tally connectivity failure the endpoint returns HTTP 200 with success=False\n171\t    and error_message set, so the UI can show the error without treating it as a\n172\t    server crash.\n173\t    \"\"\"\n174\t    try:\n175\t        log = )\n104\t\n105\t\n106\tdef _invoice_line_plan(\n107\t    invoice: Invoice, *, is_sales: bool, main_purpose: str\n108\t) -> list[tuple[str, VoucherSide, Decimal, str]]:\n109\t    \"\"\"The ordered (purpose, side, amount, line_type) a voucher gets.\n110\t\n111\t    Shared by the builder and by `refresh_invoice_voucher_ledgers`, so a repair\n112\t    maps an existing line back to the purpose that is never\n157\t    replaced, and only while the voucher is still editable. Amounts and sides\n158\t    are untouched: no line is added, removed or rebalanced. Returns the number\n159\t    of lines filled.\n160\t    \"\"\"\n161\t    if voucher.status not in (VoucherStatus.DRAFT, VoucherStatus.NEEDS_REVIEW):\n162\t        return 0\n163\t    if not any(line.ledger_id is None for line in voucher.lines):\n164\t        return 0\n165\t\n166\t    invoice = db.get(Invoice, voucher.source_id)\n167\t    if invoice is None or invoice.company_id != the plan; leave it alone.\n175\t    if len(plan) != len(lines):\n176\t        return 0\n177\t\n178\t    response = suggest_for_invoice(db, company_id, invoice.id)\n179\t    suggestions = continue\n185\t        if line.side is not side or line.amount != amount or line.line_type != line_type:\n186\t            return 0\n187\t        suggestion = suggestions.get(purpose)\n188\t        if      field=issue.field,\\n 66\\t            severity=issue.severity,\\n 67\\t        )\\n 68\\t        for issue in response.exceptions\\n 69\\t    ]\\n 70\\t\\n 71\\t\\n 72\\tdef billed_quantity=item.quantity if item is not None else None,\\n101\\t        item_amount=item.taxable_amount if item is not None else None,\\n102\\t        tax_type=None,\\n103\\t \\\"\\\"\\\"The ordered (purpose, side, amount, line_type) a voucher gets.\\n110\\t\\n111\\t    Shared by the builder and by `refresh_invoice_voucher_ledgers`, so a repair\\n112\\t    maps an existing line back to the purpose that created it instead of\\n113\\t    re-guessing from the amount — CGST and SGST are routinely equal.\\n114\\t    Zero and missing amounts produce no line, exactly as the builder does.\\n115\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n116\\t    tax_side = VoucherSide.CR if is_sales else VoucherSide.DR\\n117\\t    plan: list[tuple[str, VoucherSide, Decimal | None, str]] = []\\n118\\t    if is_sales:\\n119\\t        plan.append((\\\"PARTY\\\", VoucherSide.DR, invoice.total_amount, \\\"LEDGER\\\"))\\n120\\t        plan.append((main_purpose, VoucherSide.CR, invoice.taxable_amount, \\\"LEDGER\\\"))\\n121\\t    else:\\n122\\t        plan.append((main_purpose, VoucherSide.DR, invoice.taxable_amount, \\\"LEDGER\\\"))\\n123\\t\\n124\\t    for purpose, amount in (\\n125\\t        (\\\"CGST\\\", invoice.cgst_amount),\\n126\\t        (\\\"SGST\\\", invoice.sgst_amount),\\n127\\t        (\\\"IGST\\\", invoice.igst_amount),\\n128\\t        (\\\"CESS\\\", invoice.cess_amount),\\n129\\t    ):\\n130\\t        plan.append((purpose, tax_side, amount, \\\"TAX\\\"))\\n131\\t\\n132\\t    if invoice.round_off is not None and invoice.round_off != ZERO:\\n133\\t        if is_sales:\\n134\\t            side = VoucherSide.CR if invoice.round_off > ZERO else VoucherSide.DR\\n135\\t        else:\\n136\\t            side = VoucherSide.DR if invoice.round_off > ZERO else VoucherSide.CR\\n137\\t        plan.append((\\\"ROUND_OFF\\\", side, abs(invoice.round_off), \\\"LEDGER\\\"))\\n138\\t\\n139\\t    if not is_sales:\\n140\\t        plan.append((\\\"PARTY\\\", VoucherSide.CR, invoice.total_amount, \\\"LEDGER\\\"))\\n141\\t\\n142\\t    return [\\n143\\t        (purpose, side, amount, line_type)\\n144\\t        for purpose, side, amount, line_type in plan\\n145\\t        if amount is not None and amount != ZERO\\n146\\t    ]\\n147\\t\\n148\\t\\n149\\tdef refresh_invoice_voucher_ledgers(\\n150\\t    db: Session, company_id: uuid.UUID, voucher: Voucher\\n151\\t) -> int:\\n152\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"Fill a draft's unresolved ledger lines from today's suggestions.\\n153\\t\\n154\\t    A voucher built before its mapping existed keeps an empty ledger line\\n155\\t    forever, because creation is idempotent and returns the existing draft.\\n156\\t    This fills only lines with no ledger, so a reviewer's own choice is never\\n157\\t    replaced, and only while the voucher is still editable. Amounts and sides\\n158\\t    are untouched: no line is added, removed or rebalanced. Returns the number\\n159\\t    of lines filled.\\n160\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n161\\t    if voucher.status not in (VoucherStatus.DRAFT, VoucherStatus.NEEDS_REVIEW):\\n162\\t        return 0\\n163\\t    if not any(line.ledger_id is None for line in voucher.lines):\\n164\\t        return 0\\n165\\t\\n166\\t    invoice = db.get(Invoice, voucher.source_id)\\n167\\t    if invoice is None or invoice.company_id != company_id:\\n168\\t        return 0\\n169\\t\\n170\\t    is_sales = invoice.direction is InvoiceDirection.SALES\\n171\\t    main_purpose = \\\"SALES\\\" if is_sales else \\\"EXPENSE\\\"\\n172\\t    plan = _invoice_line_plan(invoice, is_sales=is_sales, main_purpose=main_purpose)\\n173\\t    lines = sorted(voucher.lines, key=lambda line: line.line_number)\\n174\\t    # A hand-edited voucher no longer matches the plan; leave it alone.\\n175\\t    if len(plan) != len(lines):\\n176\\t        return 0\\n177\\t\\n178\\t    response = suggest_for_invoice(db, company_id, invoice.id)\\n179\\t    suggestions = _suggestion_map(response)\\n180\\t\\n181\\t    filled = 0\\n182\\t    for (purpose, side, amount, line_type), line in zip(plan, lines):\\n183\\t        if line.ledger_id is not None:\\n184\\t            continue\\n185\\t        if line.side is not side or line.amount != amount or line.line_type != line_type:\\n186\\t            return 0\\n187\\t        suggestion = suggestions.get(purpose)\\n188\\t        if suggestion is None or suggestion.ledger_id is None:\\n189\\t            continue\\n190\\t        line.ledger_id = suggestion.ledger_id\\n191\\t        line.ledger_name = suggestion.ledger_name or \\\"\\\"\\n192\\t        filled += 1\\n193\\t\\n194\\t    if not filled:\\n195\\t        return 0\\n196\\t\\n197\\t    db.flush()\\n198\\t    issues = persist_validation(db, voucher, extra=_issues_from_response(response))\\n199\\t    # Re-derived from the filled lines: a voucher whose only complaint was the\\n200\\t    # missing ledger returns to DRAFT, one that still fails stays in review.\\n201\\t    voucher.status = VoucherStatus.NEEDS_REVIEW if issues else VoucherStatus.DRAFT\\n202\\t    record_event(\\n203\\t        db,\\n204\\t        company_id=company_id,\\n205\\t        entity_type=AccountingEntityType.VOUCHER,\\n206\\t        entity_id=voucher.id,\\n207\\t        voucher_id=voucher.id,\\n208\\t        event_type=\\\"VOUCHER_LEDGERS_RESUGGESTED\\\",\\n209\\t        actor=\\\"local-user\\\",\\n210\\t        previous_status=VoucherStatus.NEEDS_REVIEW.value,\\n211\\t        new_status=voucher.status.value,\\n212\\t        metadata={\\n213\\t            \\\"lines_filled\\\": filled,\\n214\\t            \\\"issues\\\": issues_as_dicts(issues),\\n215\\t        },\\n216\\t    )\\n217\\t    db.commit()\\n218\\t    db.refresh(voucher)\\n219\\t    return filled\\n220\\t\\n221\\t\\n222\\tdef _existing_source_voucher(\\n223\\t    db: Session,\\n224\\t    company_id: voucher_id=voucher.id,\\n277\\t        event_type=event_type,\\n278\\t        actor=actor,\\n279\\t        previous_status=None,\\n280\\t        new_status=voucher.status.value,\\n281\\t {"content":"  1\t\"\"\"Explainable ledger suggestions for invoices and bank transactions.\n  2\t\n  3\tThe suggestion engine is deliberately conservative. It only returns a ledger\n  4\twhen there is traceable evidence:\n  5\t\n  6\t* an approved company mapping;\n  7\t* a previously approved voucher for the same vendor;\n  8\t* a configured vendor/company ledger name; or\n  9\t* the confirmed bank counterparty ledger.\n 10\t\n 11\tWhen none exists it records `NO_HISTORICAL_MAPPING` and, for the main\n 12\texpense/sales line only, asks the AI to pre-select one of the company's\n 13\texisting ledgers (see `app.services.ledger_ai_suggestion`). That choice is\n 14\tflagged `AI_SUGGESTED_LEDGER` and keeps the voucher in `NEEDS_REVIEW`, so a\n 15\treviewer still confirms it. It never invents a ledger, tax rate or amount:\n 16\ttax, party and round-off ledgers remain configuration-only.\n 17\t\"\"\"\n 18\t\n 19\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n 20\t\n 21\timport uuid\n 22\tfrom decimal import Decimal\n 23\t\n 24\tfrom sqlalchemy import or_, select\n 25\tfrom sqlalchemy.orm import Session\n 26\t\n 27\tfrom app.models.accounting_mapping import (\n 28\t    TALLY_HISTORY_CONTEXT,\n 29\t    AccountingMapping,\n 30\t    AccountingMappingMatchType,\n 31\t    AccountingMappingSourceType,\n 32\t)\n 33\tfrom app.models.bank import BankAccount, BankStatement, BankTransaction\n 34\tfrom app.models.company import Company\n 35\tfrom app.models.invoice import Invoice, InvoiceDirection\n 36\tfrom app.models.invoice_line_item import InvoiceLineItem\n 37\tfrom app.models.ledger import Ledger\n 38\tfrom app.models.vendor import Vendor\n 39\tfrom app.models.voucher import (\n 40\t    Voucher,\n 41\t    VoucherSide,\n 42\t    VoucherSourceType,\n 43\t    VoucherStatus,\n 44\t    VoucherType,\n 45\t)\n 46\tfrom app.schemas.voucher import (\n 47\t    LedgerSuggestion,\n 48\t    LedgerSuggestionResponse,\n 49\t    VoucherIssueResponse,\n 50\t)\n 51\tfrom app.services.bank import (\n 52\t    BankAccountNotFoundError,\n 53\t    BankStatementNotFoundError,\n 54\t    get_transaction,\n 55\t)\n 56\tfrom app.services.company import get_company\n 57\tfrom app.services.invoice import InvoiceNotFoundError, get_invoice\n 58\tfrom app.services.ledger import find_ledger_by_name, list_ledgers\n 59\tfrom app.services.ledger_ai_suggestion import suggest_ledger_with_ai\n 60\t\n 61\tZERO = Decimal(\"0\")\n 62\t\n 63\t#: Historical states whose ledger choices are safe to reuse as evidence.\n 64\t_HISTORICAL_STATUSES = (\n 65\t    VoucherStatus.APPROVED,\n 66\t    VoucherStatus.AWAITING_IMPORT_CONFIRMATION,\n 67\t    VoucherStatus.POSTED,\n 68\t)\n 69\t\n 70\t\n 71\tdef _issue(code: str, message: str, field: str | None = None) -> VoucherIssueResponse:\n 72\t    return VoucherIssueResponse(code=code, message=message, field=field)\n 73\t\n 74\t\n 75\tdef _configured_suggestion(\n 76\t    db: Session,\n 77\t    company_id: uuid.UUID,\n 78\t    *,\n 79\t    purpose: str,\n 80\t    name: str | None,\n 81\t    source: str,\n 82\t    reason: str,\n 83\t    exceptions: list[VoucherIssueResponse],\n 84\t    missing_code: str = \"MISSING_LEDGER\",\n 85\t    field: str | None = None,\n 86\t) -> LedgerSuggestion | None:\n 87\t    \"\"\"Resolve a configured ledger name without inventing a master record.\"\"\"\n 88\t    if not name or not name.strip():\n 89\t        return None\n 90\t    ledger = find_ledger_by_name(db, company_id, name)\n 91\t    if ledger is None:\n 92\t        exceptions.append(\n 93\t            _issue(\n 94\t                missing_code,\n 95\t                (\n 96\t                    f\"Configured ledger '{name}' is not in this company's Ledger \"\n 97\t                    \"Master. Select or create the ledger before approval.\"\n 98\t                ),\n 99\t                field or purpose,\n100\t            )\n101\t        )\n102\t        return LedgerSuggestion(\n103\t            purpose=purpose,\n104\t            ledger_id=None,\n105\t            ledger_name=name,\n106\t            source=source,\n107\t            reason=reason,\n108\t            code=missing_code,\n109\t        )\n110\t    return LedgerSuggestion(\n111\t        purpose=purpose,\n112\t        ledger_id=ledger.id,\n113\t        ledger_name=ledger.name,\n114\t        source=source,\n115\t        reason=reason,\n116\t    )\n117\t\n118\t\n119\tdef _ledger_suggestion(\n120\t    db: Session,\n121\t    company_id: uuid.UUID,\n122\t    *,\n123\t    purpose: str,\n124\t    ledger_id: uuid.UUID,\n125\t    source: str,\n126\t    reason: str,\n127\t    exceptions: list[VoucherIssueResponse],\n128\t) -> LedgerSuggestion | None:\n129\t    ledger = db.scalar(\n130\t        select(Ledger).where(\n131\t            Ledger.id == ledger_id, Ledger.company_id == company_id\n132\t        )\n133\t    )\n134\t    if ledger is None or not ledger.is_active:\n135\t        exceptions.append(\n136\t            _issue(\n137\t                \"MISSING_LEDGER\",\n138\t                f\"The suggested ledger for {purpose} is not an active company ledger.\",\n139\t                purpose,\n140\t            )\n141\t        )\n142\t        return None\n143\t    return LedgerSuggestion(\n144\t        purpose=purpose,\n145\t        ledger_id=ledger.id,\n146\t        ledger_name=ledger.name,\n147\t        source=source,\n148\t        reason=reason,\n149\t    )\n150\t\n151\t\n152\tdef _mapping_evidence(\n153\t    db: Session,\n154\t    company_id: uuid.UUID,\n155\t    *,\n156\t    source_type: AccountingMappingSourceType,\n157\t    voucher_type: VoucherType,\n158\t    vendor_id: uuid.UUID | None,\n159\t    counterparty_name: str | None,\n160\t    narration: str | None,\n161\t) -> tuple[list[AccountingMapping], bool]:\n162\t    \"\"\"Return active mappings matching the context and whether there is ambiguity.\"\"\"\n163\t    query = select(AccountingMapping).where(\n164\t        AccountingMapping.company_id == company_id,\n165\t        AccountingMapping.source_type == source_type,\n166\t        AccountingMapping.is_active.is_(True),\n167\t        or_(\n168\t            AccountingMapping.voucher_type.is_(None),\n169\t            AccountingMapping.voucher_type == voucher_type.value,\n170\t        ),\n171\t    )\n172\t    evidence = []\n173\t    if vendor_id is not None:\n174\t        evidence.append(\n175\t            (\n176\t                AccountingMappingMatchType.VENDOR,\n177\t                AccountingMapping.vendor_id == vendor_id,\n178\t            )\n179\t        )\n180\t    if counterparty_name:\n181\t        evidence.append(\n182\t            (\n183\t                AccountingMappingMatchType.COUNTERPARTY,\n184\t                AccountingMapping.counterparty_name.ilike(counterparty_name.strip()),\n185\t            )\n186\t        )\n187\t    if narration:\n188\t        evidence.append(\n189\t            (\n190\t                AccountingMappingMatchType.NARRATION,\n191\t                AccountingMapping.narration_pattern.ilike(f\"%{narration.strip()}%\"),\n192\t            )\n193\t        )\n194\t    if not evidence:\n195\t        return [], False\n196\t\n197\t    # One query per evidence kind keeps the SQL readable and lets the caller\n198\t    # distinguish identity matches from narration rules.\n199\t    found: list[AccountingMapping] = []\n200\t    for _match_type, condition in evidence:\n201\t        rows = list(db.scalars(query.where(condition).limit(20)).all())\n202\t        found.extend(rows)\n203\t    unique = {mapping.id: mapping for mapping in {"content":" 1\t\"\"\"Application settings, loaded from environment variables and an optional .env file.\n 2\t\n 3\tThis module is the single source of truth for configuration. Secrets are never\n 4\thardcoded here: values come from the process environment, falling back to the\n 5\tlocal `.env` file (see `.env.example`).\n 6\t\"\"\"\n 7\t\n 8\tfrom functools import lru_cache\n 9\tfrom pathlib import Path\n10\t\n11\tfrom pydantic import Field\n12\tfrom pydantic_settings import BaseSettings, SettingsConfigDict\n13\t\n14\t\n15\tclass Settings(BaseSettings):\n16\t    \"\"\"Runtime configuration for the PAL backend.\"\"\"\n17\t\n18\t    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(\n19\t        env_file=\".env\",\n20\t        env_file_encoding=\"utf-8\",\n21\t        case_sensitive=False,\n22\t        extra=\"ignore\",\n23\t    )\n24\t\n25\t    # --- Application ---------------------------------------------------\n26\t    app_name: str = \"PAL Accounting Automation API\"\n27\t    app_env: str = \"development\"\n28\t    debug: bool = True\n29\t\n30\t    # --- Database ------------------------------------------------------\n31\t    # SQLAlchemy 2.x URL using the psycopg (v3) driver.\n32\t    database_url: str = \"postgresql+psycopg://postgres:postgres@localhost:5432/pal_db\"\n33\t\n34\t    # --- API -----------------------------------------------------------\n35\t    api_v1_prefix: str = \"/api/v1\"\n36\t\n37\t    # --- CORS ----------------------------------------------------------\n38\t    # Comma-separated list of allowed origins, e.g. \"http://localhost:5173\".\n39\t    cors_origins: str = \"http://localhost:5173\"\n40\t\n41\t    # --- Document storage ----------------------------------------------\n42\t    # PAL runs inside the client's own environment, so uploaded documents are\n43\t    # kept on local disk. A relative path resolves against the process working\n44\t    # directory (the backend/ folder). Never point this at a shared or\n45\t    # unencrypted location on a multi-user machine.\n46\t    document_storage_path: str = \"./storage/documents\"\n47\t    # Uploads above this size are rejected before anything touches disk.\n48\t    document_max_upload_bytes: int = 20 * 1024 * 1024  # 20 MB\n49\t\n50\t    # --- AI Provider (DeepSeek) ----------------------------------------\n51\t    # API key is NEVER logged, returned, or exposed to the frontend.\n52\t    deepseek_api_key: str = \"\"\n53\t    deepseek_base_url: str = \"https://api.deepseek.com\"\n54\t    deepseek_model: str = \"deepseek-flash\"\n55\t\n56\t    # --- Invoice extraction --------------------------------------------\n57\t    # Hard cap on PDF pages sent to the vision model. A document above this\n58\t    # limit is rejected for review rather than silently truncated.\n59\t    invoice_max_pages: int = 10\n60\t\n61\t    # --- TallyPrime integration (read-only POC) -------------------------\n62\t    # The URL is server-side configuration only. It is never accepted from a\n63\t    # request, so a caller cannot turn PAL into an open proxy. TallyPrime's\n64\t    # XML-over-HTTP server normally listens on port 9000.\n65\t    tally_base_url: str = \"http://localhost:9000\"\n66\t    # Bounded so a dead TallyPrime cannot hold a PAL request open indefinitely.\n67\t    tally_timeout_seconds: float = Field(default=10.0, gt=0, le=60)\n68\t\n69\t    @property\n70\t    def document_storage_dir(self) -> Path:\n71\t        \"\"\"Absolute, expanded storage root for uploaded documents.\"\"\"\n72\t        return Path(self.document_storage_path).expanduser().resolve()\n73\t\n74\t    @property\n75\t    def cors_origins_list(self) -> list[str]:\n76\t        \"\"\"CORS origins parsed into a list, for CORSMiddleware.\"\"\"\n77\t        return [origin.strip() for origin in self.cors_origins.split(\",\") if origin.strip()]\n78\t\n79\t    @property\n80\t    def is_production(self) -> bool:\n81\t        \"\"\"True when running against the production environment.\"\"\"\n82\t        return self.app_env.lower() == \"production\"\n83\t\n84\t    @property\n85\t    def ai_configured(self) -> bool:\n86\t        \"\"\"Whether an AI provider is configured and usable.\"\"\"\n87\t        return bool(self.deepseek_api_key and self.deepseek_api_key.strip())\n88\t\n89\t\n90\t@lru_cache\n91\tdef get_settings() -> Settings:\n92\t    \"\"\"Return the cached Settings instance.\"\"\"\n93\t    return Settings()","bytes":3846,"startLine":1,"endLine":93,"totalLines":93}

## tool

{"content":"  1\t# TallyPrime Read-Only Integration POC\n  2\t\n  3\tThis POC proves that the PAL backend can reach TallyPrime's XML-over-HTTP\n  4\tserver, read the loaded company, and read its ledgers. It does **not** create,\n  5\tedit, map, import or post vouchers.\n  6\t\n  7\t## 1. Configuration\n  8\t\n  9\tAdd these values to `backend/.env` (or set them in the process environment):\n 10\t\n 11\t```env\n 12\tTALLY_BASE_URL=http://localhost:9000\n 13\tTALLY_TIMEOUT_SECONDS=10\n 14\t```\n 15\t\n 16\t`TALLY_BASE_URL` is read only from server-side configuration. No API caller\n 17\tcan supply a Tally URL. `TALLY_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` must be greater than `0` and no\n 18\tmore than `60`.\n 19\t\n 20\t## 2. Start TallyPrime\n 21\t\n 22\t1. Open the company to be read.\n 23\t2. Configure TallyPrime as a server:\n 24\t   - **TallyPrime acts as:** Server\n 25\t   - **Port:** 9000\n 26\t   - **Enable ODBC:** Yes\n 27\t3. Keep TallyPrime running while PAL is tested.\n 28\t\n 29\t## 3. Verify TallyPrime directly\n 30\t\n 31\tOpen:\n 32\t\n 33\t```text\n 34\thttp://localhost:9000\n 35\t```\n 36\t\n 37\tExpected response:\n 38\t\n 39\t```xml\n 40\t<RESPONSE>TallyPrime Server is Running</RESPONSE>\n 41\t```\n 42\t\n 43\t## 4. Start the PAL backend\n 44\t\n 45\tFrom the `backend` directory:\n 46\t\n 47\t```sh\n 48\t.\\.venv\\Scripts\\python.exe -m uvicorn app.main:app --host 127.0.0.1 --port 8082\n 49\t```\n 50\t\n 51\tSwagger UI: `http://127.0.0.1:8082/docs`\n 52\t\n 53\t## 5. API calls\n 54\t\n 55\t### Connection status\n 56\t\n 57\t```http\n 58\tGET /api/v1/tally/status\n 59\t```\n 60\t\n 61\tThe endpoint makes a real company-list request to TallyPrime. A disconnected\n 62\tTallyPrime is reported as HTTP 200 with `connected: false`.\n 63\t\n 64\t```json\n 65\t{\n 66\t  \"connected\": true,\n 67\t  \"url\": \"http://localhost:9000\",\n 68\t  \"message\": \"TallyPrime is reachable\"\n 69\t}\n 70\t```\n 71\t\n 72\t### Companies\n 73\t\n 74\t```http\n 75\tGET /api/v1/tally/companies\n 76\t```\n 77\t\n 78\t```json\n 79\t{\n 80\t  \"success\": true,\n 81\t  \"companies\": [\n 82\t    { \"name\": \"PAL Test Company\" }\n 83\t  ],\n 84\t  \"message\": null\n 85\t}\n 86\t```\n 87\t\n 88\tIf no company is loaded:\n 89\t\n 90\t```json\n 91\t{\n 92\t  \"success\": true,\n 93\t  \"companies\": [],\n 94\t  \"message\": \"No company is loaded in TallyPrime.\"\n 95\t}\n 96\t```\n 97\t\n 98\t### Sync companies into PAL\n 99\t\n100\t```http\n101\tPOST /api/v1/tally/companies/sync\n102\t```\n103\t\n104\tReads the TallyPrime company collection and upserts each company into PAL's\n105\tcompany master. Matching is case-insensitive, and existing companies are never\n106\thard-deleted. A company archived locally (`INACTIVE`) is restored to `ACTIVE`\n107\ton the next sync if it still exists in Tally, because Tally is the source of\n108\ttruth. The **Sync from Tally** button on the Companies page calls this endpoint.\n109\t\n110\t```json\n111\t{\n112\t  \"success\": true,\n113\t  \"companies_fetched\": 1,\n114\t  \"companies_created\": 0,\n115\t  \"companies_updated\": 1,\n116\t  \"companies_unchanged\": 0,\n117\t  \"error_message\": null\n118\t}\n119\t```\n120\t\n121\t### Ledgers\n122\t\n123\t```http\n124\tGET /api/v1/tally/ledgers\n125\t```\n126\t\n127\tWhen no company is supplied, PAL uses the first company returned by Tally.\n128\tUse the optional query parameter to select a company explicitly:\n129\t\n130\t```http\n131\tGET /api/v1/tally/ledgers?company=PAL%20Test%20Company\n132\t```\n133\t\n134\t```json\n135\t{\n136\t  \"success\": true,\n137\t  \"company\": \"PAL Test Company\",\n138\t  \"ledgers\": [\n139\t    { \"name\": \"Cash\", \"parent\": \"Cash-in-Hand\" },\n140\t    { \"name\": \"HDFC Bank\", \"parent\": \"Capital Account\" }\n141\t  ],\n142\t  \"message\": null\n143\t}\n144\t```\n145\t\n146\t## 6. XML requests used\n147\t\n148\tThe exact requests are isolated in\n149\t`backend/app/integrations/tally/xml.py`:\n150\t\n151\t- `build_company_collection_request()` — `Export` / `Collection` /\n152\t  `List of Companies`, type `Company`.\n153\t- `build_ledger_collection_request(company)` — `Export` / `Collection` /\n154\t  `Ledgers`, type `Ledger`, fetching `Name` and `Parent`, optionally scoped\n155\t  with `SVCURRENTCOMPANY`.\n156\t\n157\tResponses are parsed defensively in\n158\t`backend/app/integrations/tally/parser.py`. The parser handles Tally's\n159\tnon-XML-1.0 character references such as `&#4; Primary` and UTF-16 bodies.\n160\t\n161\t## 7. Tests\n162\t\n163\tNormal tests mock HTTP and never need TallyPrime:\n164\t\n165\t```sh\n166\tcd backend\n167\t.\\.venv\\Scripts\\python.exe -m pytest tests/test_tally.py -q\n168\t```\n169\t\n170\tOptional live smoke test, with TallyPrime running:\n171\t\n172\t```powershell\n173\t$env:TALLY_LIVE_TEST=\"1\"\n174\t.\\.venv\\Scripts\\python.exe -m pytest tests/test_tally_live.py -q\n175\t```\n176\t\n177\t## 8. Troubleshooting\n178\t\n179\t| Symptom | Check |\n180\t| --- | --- |\n181\t| `Unable to connect to TallyPrime` | Is TallyPrime open and running as a server? |\n182\t| Port 9000 unavailable | Close the other process, or change `TALLY_BASE_URL` and Tally's port together. |\n183\t| Wrong URL | `TALLY_BASE_URL` must be an absolute `http://` or `https://` URL with no credentials, query or fragment. |\n184\t| No company loaded | Open the company in TallyPrime, then call `/companies` again. |\n185\t| XML parsing failure | Check that TallyPrime is returning an `ENVELOPE` response. The API returns a safe message; Tally's error text is written to the backend log at warning level, never to the caller. |\n186\t| Timeout | Increase `TALLY_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` up to 60, or check TallyPrime responsiveness. |\n187\t\n188\t## 9. Scope and next step\n189\t\n190\tImplemented:\n191\t\n192\t- read-only connectivity check;\n193\t- company read;\n194\t- ledger read;\n195\t- safe error mapping and timeouts;\n196\t- mocked unit/endpoint tests plus an opt-in live smoke test.\n197\t\n198\tNot implemented:\n199\t\n200\t- voucher creation, import or posting;\n201\t- ledger creation or PAL-to-Tally ledger mapping;\n202\t- Zoho;\n203\t- MCP;\n204\t- persistence of Tally configuration or read results;\n205\t- authentication/RBAC.\n206\t\n207\tThe next step for the first voucher-posting POC is a separate write workflow:\n208\tbuild a Tally `Import` / `Data` / `Vouchers` XML request for one approved\n209\tvoucher, add an explicit idempotency key and a dry-run mode, capture Tally's\n210\tper-voucher import result, and only then allow PAL's existing\n211\t`AWAITING_IMPORT_CONFIRMATION` -> `POSTED` verification transition with an\n212\texternal reference.","bytes":5306,"startLine":1,"endLine":212,"totalLines":212}
[trace revision=32 evidence=E0026]

XML requests used\\n147\\t\\n148\\tThe exact requests are isolated in\\n149\\t`backend/app/integrations/tally/xml.py`:\\n150\\t\\n151\\t- `build_company_collection_request()` — `Export` / `Collection` /\\n152\\t  `List of Companies`, type `Company`.\\n153\\t- `build_ledger_collection_request(company)` — `Export` / `Collection` /\\n154\\t  `Ledgers`, type `Ledger`, fetching `Name` and `Parent`, optionally scoped\\n155\\t  with `SVCURRENTCOMPANY`.\\n156\\t\\n157\\tResponses are parsed defensively in\\n158\\t`backend/app/integrations/tally/parser.py`. The parser handles Tally's\\n159\\tnon-XML-1.0 character references such as `&#4; Primary` and UTF-16 bodies.\\n160\\t\\n161\\t## 7. Tests\\n162\\t\\n163\\tNormal tests mock HTTP and never need TallyPrime:\\n164\\t\\n165\\t```sh\\n166\\tcd backend\\n167\\t.\\\\.venv\\\\Scripts\\\\python.exe -m pytest tests/test_tally.py -q\\n168\\t```\\n169\\t\\n170\\tOptional live smoke test, with TallyPrime running:\\n171\\t\\n172\\t```powershell\\n173\\t$env:TALLY_LIVE_TEST=\\\"1\\\"\\n174\\t.\\\\.venv\\\\Scripts\\\\python.exe -m pytest tests/test_tally_live.py -q\\n175\\t```\\n176\\t\\n177\\t## 8. Troubleshooting\\n178\\t\\n179\\t| Symptom | Check |\\n180\\t| --- | --- |\\n181\\t| `Unable to connect to TallyPrime` | Is TallyPrime open and running as a server? |\\n182\\t| Port 9000 unavailable | Close the other process, or change `TALLY_BASE_URL` and Tally's port together. |\\n183\\t| Wrong URL | `TALLY_BASE_URL` must be an absolute `http://` or `https://` URL with no credentials, query or fragment. |\\n184\\t| No company loaded | Open the company in TallyPrime, then call `/companies` again. |\\n185\\t| XML parsing failure | Check that TallyPrime is returning an `ENVELOPE` response. The API returns a safe message; Tally's error text is written to the backend log at warning level, never to the caller. |\\n186\\t| Timeout | Increase `TALLY_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` up to 60, or check TallyPrime responsiveness. |\\n187\\t\\n188\\t## 9. Scope and next step\\n189\\t\\n190\\tImplemented:\\n191\\t\\n192\\t- read-only connectivity check;\\n193\\t- company read;\\n194\\t- ledger read;\\n195\\t- safe error mapping and timeouts;\\n196\\t- mocked unit/endpoint tests plus an opt-in live smoke test.\\n197\\t\\n198\\tNot implemented:\\n199\\t\\n200\\t- voucher creation, import or posting;\\n201\\t- ledger creation or PAL-to-Tally ledger mapping;\\n202\\t- Zoho;\\n203\\t- MCP;\\n204\\t- persistence of Tally configuration or read results;\\n205\\t- authentication/RBAC.\\n206\\t\\n207\\tThe next step for the first voucher-posting POC is a separate write workflow:\\n208\\tbuild a Tally `Import` / `Data` / `Vouchers` XML request for one approved\\n209\\tvoucher, add an explicit idempotency key and a dry-run mode, capture Tally's\\n210\\tper-voucher import result, and only then allow PAL's existing\\n211\\t`AWAITING_IMPORT_CONFIRMATION` -> `POSTED` verification transition with an\\n212\\texternal reference.\",\"bytes\":5306,\"startLine\":1,\"endLine\":212,\"totalLines\":212}\n[trace revision=32 evidence=E0026]\n[trace writeRevision=31]"} -->

{"content":"  1\t\"\"\"Shared pytest fixtures.\n  2\t\n  3\tTests run against a real PostgreSQL database (`pal_test_db` by default, override\n  4\twith `TEST_DATABASE_URL`) rather than SQLite, so the schema under test is the\n  5\texact one Alembic manages. The dev database is never touched.\n  6\t\n  7\tUploads are redirected to a temporary directory for the whole session, so\n  8\trunning the suite can never write into the project's real `storage/` folder or\n  9\tdelete a developer's files.\n 10\t\"\"\"\n 11\t\n 12\timport os\n 13\timport shutil\n 14\tfrom collections.abc import Iterator\n 15\tfrom pathlib import Path\n 16\t\n 17\timport pytest\n 18\tfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n 19\tfrom sqlalchemy import create_engine\n 20\tfrom sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker\n 21\t\n 22\t# Registers every ORM model on Base.metadata, which is what create_all() builds\n 23\t# from. Imported explicitly rather than relying on app.main pulling it in.\n 24\timport app.models  # noqa: F401  (imported for its registration side effect)\n 25\tfrom app.core.config import get_settings\n 26\tfrom app.core.database import Base, get_db\n 27\tfrom app.main import app\n 28\t\n 29\tTEST_DATABASE_URL = os.getenv(\n 30\t    \"TEST_DATABASE_URL\",\n 31\t    \"postgresql+psycopg://postgres:postgres@localhost:5432/pal_test_db\",\n 32\t)\n 33\t\n 34\ttest_engine = create_engine(TEST_DATABASE_URL)\n 35\tTestingSessionLocal = sessionmaker(\n 36\t    bind=test_engine, autoflush=False, autocommit=False, expire_on_commit=False\n 37\t)\n 38\t\n 39\t\n 40\t@pytest.fixture(scope=\"session\", autouse=True)\n 41\tdef temp_storage(tmp_path_factory: pytest.TempPathFactory) -> Iterator[Path]:\n 42\t    \"\"\"Point document storage at a throwaway directory for the test session.\"\"\"\n 43\t    previous = os.environ.get(\"DOCUMENT_STORAGE_PATH\")\n 44\t    root = tmp_path_factory.mktemp(\"pal-document-storage\")\n 45\t    os.environ[\"DOCUMENT_STORAGE_PATH\"] = str(root)\n 46\t    get_settings.cache_clear()\n 47\t    try:\n 48\t        yield root\n 49\t    finally:\n 50\t        if previous is None:\n 51\t            os.environ.pop(\"DOCUMENT_STORAGE_PATH\", None)\n 52\t        else:\n 53\t            os.environ[\"DOCUMENT_STORAGE_PATH\"] = previous\n 54\t        get_settings.cache_clear()\n 55\t\n 56\t\n 57\t@pytest.fixture(scope=\"session\", autouse=True)\n 58\tdef _schema() -> Iterator[None]:\n 59\t    \"\"\"Create the tables once per test session and drop them afterwards.\"\"\"\n 60\t    Base.metadata.create_all(bind=test_engine)\n 61\t    yield\n 62\t    Base.metadata.drop_all(bind=test_engine)\n 63\t\n 64\t\n 65\t@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)\n 66\tdef no_real_ai_key(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> Iterator[None]:\n 67\t    \"\"\"Keep the real AI provider out of every test by default.\n 68\t\n 69\t    A developer's local `.env` may contain a real key. Overriding the variable\n 70\t    with an empty string takes precedence over the dotenv file, so no test can\n 71\t    accidentally spend money or depend on network access. A test that needs a\n 72\t    key sets its own with `monkeypatch.setenv` after this fixture runs.\n 73\t    \"\"\"\n 74\t    monkeypatch.setenv(\"DEEPSEEK_API_KEY\", \"\")\n 75\t    # Deterministic page-limit behaviour regardless of a developer's .env.\n 76\t    monkeypatch.setenv(\"INVOICE_MAX_PAGES\", \"10\")\n 77\t    get_settings.cache_clear()\n 78\t    try:\n 79\t        yield\n 80\t    finally:\n 81\t        get_settings.cache_clear()\n 82\t\n 83\t\n 84\t@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)\n 85\tdef clean_state(temp_storage: Path) -> Iterator[None]:\n 86\t    \"\"\"Empty the tables and the temp storage root before every test.\n 87\t\n 88\t    Uploading is a filesystem side effect, so clearing only the database would\n 89\t    let one test's files leak into the next one's assertions.\n 90\t    \"\"\"\n 91\t    with test_engine.begin() as connection:\n 92\t        for table in reversed(Base.metadata.sorted_tables):\n 93\t            connection.execute(table.delete())\n 94\t    for child in temp_storage.iterdir():\n 95\t        if child.is_dir():\n 96\t            shutil.rmtree(child, ignore_errors=True)\n 97\t        else:\n 98\t            child.unlink()\n 99\t    yield\n100\t\n101\t\n102\t@pytest.fixture\n103\tdef db() -> Iterator[Session]:\n104\t    \"\"\"A session the app's dependency is overridden to use.\"\"\"\n105\t    session = TestingSessionLocal()\n106\t    try:\n107\t        yield session\n108\t    finally:\n109\t        session.close()\n110\t\n111\t\n112\t@pytest.fixture\n113\tdef client(db: Session) -> Iterator[TestClient]:\n114\t    \"\"\"Test client wired to the test session instead of the real engine.\"\"\"\n115\t\n116\t    def override_get_db() -> Iterator[Session]:\n117\t        yield db\n118\t\n119\t    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = override_get_db\n120\t    with TestClient(app) as test_client:\n121\t        yield test_client\n122\t    app.dependency_overrides.clear()","bytes":4175,"startLine":1,"endLine":122,"totalLines":122}

This model records the\n  4\tevidence behind a mapping instead of storing an opaque confidence score:\n  5\t\n  6\t* the source type and optional vendor/counterparty/narration context;\n  7\t* the ledger the company approved;\n  8\t* who approved it and why.\n  9\t\n 10\tA mapping is company-owned and never crosses company boundaries.\n 11\t\"\"\"\n 12\t\n 13\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n 14\t\n 15\timport enum\n 16\timport uuid\n 17\tfrom datetime import datetime\n 18\t\n 19\tfrom sqlalchemy import (\n 20\t    Boolean,\n 21\t    DateTime,\n 22\t    Enum as SAEnum,\n 23\t    ForeignKey,\n 24\t    String,\n 25\t    Text,\n 26\t    func,\n 27\t)\n 28\tfrom sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID\n 29\tfrom sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column\n 30\t\n 31\tfrom app.core.database import Base\n 32\t\n 33\t\n 34\tclass AccountingMappingSourceType(str, enum.Enum):\n 35\t    \"\"\"The kind of source document a mapping applies to.\"\"\"\n 36\t\n 37\t    INVOICE = \"INVOICE\"\n 38\t    BANK_TRANSACTION = \"BANK_TRANSACTION\"\n 39\t\n 40\t\n 41\tclass AccountingMappingMatchType(str, enum.Enum):\n 42\t    \"\"\"How a mapping is matched.\n 43\t\n 44\t    `VENDOR` and `COUNTERPARTY` are identity matches. `NARRATION` is an approved\n 45\t    text rule. `RULE` is an explicitly configured company rule. {"content":"  1\t\"\"\"Invoice extraction over a provider-agnostic, text-first pipeline.\n  2\t\n  3\tThe extractor owns the seam between PAL and an AI provider:\n  4\t\n  5\t    Document -> routing -> text / OCR / structured / vision -> AIProvider\n  6\t             -> strict schema -> deterministic validation\n  7\t\n  8\tRouting is deliberate:\n  9\t\n 10\t* digital PDFs are read from their native text layer and never rasterised;\n 11\t* scanned PDFs go native text -> OCR -> bounded page images (vision);\n 12\t* JPEG/PNG go straight to vision;\n 13\t* XLSX/XLS/DOCX are read as structured text and sent to a text model.\n 14\t\n 15\tThe provider contract is split into text and vision capabilities, so a\n 16\ttext-only provider can process digital documents and a vision-only provider can\n 17\tstill handle images. No provider-specific exception, key, prompt, local path or\n 18\traw response leaves this module.\n 19\t\n 20\tThe extractor does not write to the database. It returns an `ExtractionResult`\n 21\tand lets the invoice service apply the lifecycle and persistence rules.\n 22\t\"\"\"\n 23\t\n 24\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n 25\t\n 26\timport inspect\n 27\timport logging\n 28\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n 29\tfrom enum import Enum\n 30\tfrom typing import Protocol, runtime_checkable\n 31\t\n 32\tfrom app.core.config import get_settings\n 33\tfrom app.models.document import Document\n 34\tfrom app.services.ai import AIProvider, AIProviderError, get_ai_provider\n 35\tfrom app.services.document_errors import DocumentPreparationError\n 36\tfrom app.services.document_preparation import (\n 37\t    DetectedDocument,\n 38\t    DocumentKind,\n 39\t    prepare_vision_images_for_extension,\n 40\t    route_document,\n 41\t)\n 42\tfrom app.services.invoice_extraction_validation import (\n 43\t    InvalidExtractionOutput,\n 44\t    parse_extraction_payload,\n 45\t    validate_extraction,\n 46\t)\n 47\tfrom app.services.invoice_prompt import (\n 48\t    INVOICE_EXTRACTION_PROMPT,\n 49\t    INVOICE_EXTRACTION_SYSTEM_PROMPT,\n 50\t    INVOICE_TEXT_SYSTEM_PROMPT,\n 51\t    build_text_extraction_prompt,\n 52\t)\n 53\tfrom app.services.ocr import OCRProcessor, OCRUnavailableError, get_ocr_processor\n 54\tfrom app.services.pdf_text import combine_pages, read_pdf_text\n 55\tfrom app.services.regex_extraction import RegexExtractor, render_candidates_for_prompt\n 56\tfrom app.services.structured_documents import extract_structured_text\n 57\tfrom app.services.text_quality import PageText, assess_text_quality\n 58\t\n 59\tlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n 60\t\n 61\t#: Stable machine-readable reasons, so callers never match on prose.\n 62\tEXTRACTION_NOT_CONFIGURED = \"EXTRACTION_NOT_CONFIGURED\"\n 63\tEXTRACTION_INVALID_OUTPUT = \"EXTRACTION_INVALID_OUTPUT\"\n 64\tEXTRACTION_NEEDS_REVIEW = \"EXTRACTION_NEEDS_REVIEW\"\n 65\tEXTRACTION_INTERNAL_ERROR = \"EXTRACTION_INTERNAL_ERROR\"\n 66\tEXTRACTION_NO_TEXT = \"EXTRACTION_NO_TEXT\"\n 67\tEXTRACTION_TEXT_TOO_LARGE = \"EXTRACTION_TEXT_TOO_LARGE\"\n 68\tNOT_AN_INVOICE = \"NOT_AN_INVOICE\"\n 69\t\n 70\tAI_TIMEOUT = \"AI_TIMEOUT\"\n 71\tAI_RATE_LIMIT = \"AI_RATE_LIMIT\"\n 72\tAI_CONNECTION_ERROR = \"AI_CONNECTION_ERROR\"\n 73\tAI_PROVIDER_ERROR = \"AI_PROVIDER_ERROR\"\n 74\tAI_UNSUPPORTED_CAPABILITY = \"AI_UNSUPPORTED_CAPABILITY\"\n 75\t\n 76\tNOT_CONFIGURED_MESSAGE = (\n 77\t    \"Automatic extraction is not configured yet. Enter the invoice fields manually.\"\n 78\t)\n 79\tNO_TEXT_MESSAGE = (\n 80\t    \"No readable text was found in the document. Upload a clearer copy or enter \"\n 81\t    \"the invoice fields manually.\"\n 82\t)\n 83\tTEXT_TOO_LARGE_MESSAGE = (\n 84\t    \"The document contains too much text to process safely. Split it into \"\n 85\t    \"smaller files or enter the invoice fields manually.\"\n 86\t)\n 87\t\n 88\t#: Reasoning models (e.g. deepseek-flash) consume reasoning tokens before\n 89\t#: writing output. 2048 is exhausted entirely by chain-of-thought, leaving\n 90\t#: nothing for the JSON response. 16384 gives ample room for both.\n 91\tEXTRACTION_MAX_TOKENS = 16384\n 92\t#: Hard cap on document text sent to a text model. Ten ordinary invoice pages\n 93\t#: sit far below this. A larger payload is rejected rather than truncated: the\n 94\t#: missing tail could contain the invoice total, and a partial prompt is not a\n 95\t#: safe basis for an accounting entry.\n 96\tMAX_TEXT_PROMPT_CHARACTERS = 120_000\n 97\t\n 98\t\n 99\tclass ExtractionOutcome(str, Enum):\n100\t    \"\"\"What the extraction attempt implies for the invoice lifecycle.\"\"\"\n101\t\n102\t    PROCESSED = \"PROCESSED\"\n103\t    NEEDS_REVIEW = \"NEEDS_REVIEW\"\n104\t    FAILED = \"FAILED\"\n105\t    NOT_CONFIGURED = \"NOT_CONFIGURED\"\n106\t\n107\t\n108\tclass ExtractionMethod(str, Enum):\n109\t    \"\"\"Which processing path produced the result.\n110\t\n111\t    This is safe operational metadata, not accounting data. It lets an operator\n112\t    see why a document took a particular path without storing document content.\n113\t    \"\"\"\n114\t\n115\t    NATIVE_TEXT = \"NATIVE_TEXT\"\n116\t    OCR_TEXT = \"OCR_TEXT\"\n117\t    IMAGE_VISION = \"IMAGE_VISION\"\n118\t    EXCEL_TEXT = \"EXCEL_TEXT\"\n119\t    DOCX_TEXT = \"DOCX_TEXT\"\n120\t\n\n… 475 more line(s). Read with offset=121 to continue.","bytes":21638,"startLine":1,"endLine":120,"totalLines":595,"nextOffset":121}

For a normal\n490\t    bank account a debit increases the balance, which a bank statement calls a\n491\t    credit. An overdraft or credit-card ledger is the mirror image.\n492\t    \"\"\"\n493\t    amount = abs(entry.amount)\n494\t    if entry.is_deemed_positive:\n495\t        return (ZERO, amount) if not is_overdraft else (amount, ZERO)\n496\t    return (amount, ZERO) if not is_overdraft else (ZERO, amount)\n497\t\n498\t\n499\tdef sync_tally_bank_data(\n500\t    db: Session,\n501\t    company_id: uuid.UUID,\n502\t    tally_company_name: str,\n503\t    *,\n504\t    from_date: date | None = None,\n505\t    to_date: date | None = None,\n506\t    service: TallyService | None = None,\n507\t) -> TallyBankDataSyncResult:\n508\t    \"\"\"Import bank accounts and their bank-ledger vouchers from TallyPrime.\n509\t\n510\t    The run is additive and idempotent: accounts are matched by ledger name,\n511\t    and each voucher line is stored with a stable ``external_id`` so a second\n512\t    sync of the same period creates nothing twice. PAL never writes to Tally.\n513\t\n514\t    Raises CompanyNotFoundError when company_id is not in PAL.\n515\t    \"\"\"\n516\t    get_company(db, company_id)\n517\t\n518\t    tally_company_name =
