period_to < period_from:\n523\t        raise ValueError(\"The end date must not be before the start date.\")\n524\t\n525\t    owned_service = service is None\n526\t    svc: TallyService = service or get_tally_service()\n527\t\n528\t    accounts_fetched = accounts_created = accounts_updated = accounts_unchanged = 0\n529\t    vouchers_fetched = vouchers_matched = 0\n530\t    statements_created = transactions_created = transactions_skipped = 0\n531\t    error_message: str | None = None\n532\t\n533\t    try:\n534\t        ledger_result = svc.get_ledgers(tally_company_name)\n535\t can be decided per account rather than guessed globally.\n544\t        accounts = list(\n545\t            db.scalars(\n546\t                select(BankAccount).where(BankAccount.company_id == company_id)\n547\t            ).all()\n548\t        )\n549\t        account_by_ledger = {\n550\t            (account.ledger_name or \"\").casefold(): account\n551\t            for account in accounts\n552\t            if (account.ledger_name or \"\").strip()\n553\t        }\n554\t        group_by_ledger = {\n555\t            (ledger.name or \"\").casefold(): (ledger.parent or \"\").strip().lower()\n556\t            for ledger in bank_ledgers\n557\t            if (ledger.name or \"\").strip()\n558\t        }\n559\t\n560\t        voucher_result = {"content":"  1\t\"\"\"Bank statement extraction abstraction.\n  2\t\n  3\tThe seam a real parser plugs into. The AI extractor below uses the same\n  4\tdocument preparation pipeline as invoice extraction:\n  5\t\n  6\t    Document -> routing -> text / OCR / structured / vision -> AIProvider\n  7\t             -> JSON response -> typed transaction rows\n  8\t\n  9\tThe extractor does not write to the database. It returns a\n 10\t`StatementExtractionResult` and lets `process_statement` apply the lifecycle\n 11\tand persistence rules.\n 12\t\"\"\"\n 13\t\n 14\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n 15\t\n 16\timport inspect\n 17\timport json\n 18\timport logging\n 19\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n 20\tfrom datetime import date, datetime\n 21\tfrom decimal import Decimal, InvalidOperation\n 22\tfrom typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Protocol, runtime_checkable\n 23\t\n 24\tfrom app.models.bank import BankStatement\n 25\t\n 26\tif TYPE_CHECKING:\n 27\t    from app.models.document import Document\n 28\t\n 29\tlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n 30\t\n 31\t#: Stable machine-readable reasons, so callers never match on prose.\n 32\tEXTRACTION_NOT_CONFIGURED = \"EXTRACTION_NOT_CONFIGURED\"\n 33\tEXTRACTION_INVALID_OUTPUT = \"EXTRACTION_INVALID_OUTPUT\"\n 34\tEXTRACTION_INTERNAL_ERROR = \"EXTRACTION_INTERNAL_ERROR\"\n 35\tNOT_A_BANK_STATEMENT = \"NOT_A_BANK_STATEMENT\"\n 36\tEXTRACTION_NO_TEXT = \"EXTRACTION_NO_TEXT\"\n 37\tEXTRACTION_TEXT_TOO_LARGE = \"EXTRACTION_TEXT_TOO_LARGE\"\n 38\tAI_TIMEOUT = \"AI_TIMEOUT\"\n 39\tAI_RATE_LIMIT = \"AI_RATE_LIMIT\"\n 40\tAI_CONNECTION_ERROR = \"AI_CONNECTION_ERROR\"\n 41\tAI_PROVIDER_ERROR = \"AI_PROVIDER_ERROR\"\n 42\tAI_UNSUPPORTED_CAPABILITY = \"AI_UNSUPPORTED_CAPABILITY\"\n 43\t\n 44\tNOT_CONFIGURED_MESSAGE = (\n 45\t    \"Automatic bank statement extraction is not configured yet. \"\n 46\t    \"Add transactions manually.\"\n 47\t)\n 48\t\n 49\t#: A statement with 200 transactions × ~50 tokens each needs room to breathe.\n 50\tBANK_MAX_TOKENS = 16_384\n 51\t#: Hard cap on document text. Larger files are rejected rather than truncated\n 52\t#: to avoid cutting off mid-statement and producing a partial result.\n 53\tBANK_MAX_TEXT_CHARACTERS = 400_000\n 54\t#: Max pages to process from a PDF bank statement.\n 55\tBANK_MAX_PAGES = 20\n 56\t\n 57\t_DATE_FORMATS = (\n 58\t    \"%Y-%m-%d\",\n 59\t    \"%d/%m/%Y\",\n 60\t    \"%d-%m-%Y\",\n 61\t    \"%d.%m.%Y\",\n 62\t    \"%d/%m/%y\",\n 63\t    \"%d-%m-%y\",\n 64\t    \"%d %b %Y\",\n 65\t    \"%d %b %y\",\n 66\t    \"%d %B %Y\",\n 67\t)\n 68\t\n 69\t\n 70\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n 71\tclass StatementExtractionResult:\n 72\t    \"\"\"Outcome of an extraction attempt.\n 73\t\n 74\t    `configured` is False whenever no parser could run. An unconfigured result\n 75\t    never carries transactions.\n 76\t\n 77\t    `transactions` holds parsed transaction rows ready to persist.\n 78\t    `statement_updates` holds statement-level fields extracted from the header\n 79\t    (dates, balances) that `process_statement` may apply to the statement row.\n 80\t    \"\"\"\n 81\t\n 82\t    configured: bool\n 83\t    reason: str | None = None\n 84\t    message: str | None = None\n 85\t    transaction_count: int | None = None\n 86\t    transactions: tuple[dict, ...] = ()\n 87\t    statement_updates: dict | None = None\n 88\t    issues: tuple[str, ...] = ()\n 89\t\n 90\t\n\n… 514 more line(s). Read with offset=91 to continue.","bytes":21233,"startLine":1,"endLine":90,"totalLines":604,"nextOffset":91}

{"content":"  1\t/** React Query bindings for TallyPrime status and sync. */\n  2\t\n  3\timport { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from \"@tanstack/react-query\";\n  4\t\n  5\timport {\n  6\t  tallyApi,\n  7\t  type TallyBankDataSyncDto,\n  8\t  type TallyBankSyncDto,\n  9\t  type TallyCompanySyncDto,\n 10\t  type TallyMappingSyncDto,\n 11\t  type TallyStatusDto,\n 12\t  type TallySyncDto,\n 13\t} from \"@/lib/api/tally\";\n 14\t\n 15\tconst isBrowser = typeof window !== \"undefined\";\n 16\t\n 17\texport const tallyKeys = {\n 18\t  status: () => [\"tally\", \"status\"] as const,\n 19\t  latestSync: (companyId: string) => [\"tally\", \"sync\", companyId] as const,\n 20\t};\n 21\t\n 22\texport function useTallyStatus() {\n 23\t  return useQuery<TallyStatusDto>({\n 24\t    queryKey: tallyKeys.status(),\n 25\t    queryFn: () => tallyApi.status(),\n 26\t    enabled: isBrowser,\n 27\t    staleTime: 15_000,\n 28\t    retry: false,\n 29\t  });\n 30\t}\n 31\t\n 32\texport function useTallyCompanySync() {\n 33\t  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\n 34\t  return useMutation<TallyCompanySyncDto, Error, void>({\n 35\t    mutationFn: () => tallyApi.syncCompanies(),\n 36\t    onSuccess: () => {\n 37\t      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [\"companies\"] });\n 38\t      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: tallyKeys.status() });\n 39\t    },\n 40\t  });\n 41\t}\n 42\t\n 43\texport function useTallyLatestSync(companyId: string | undefined) {\n 44\t  return useQuery<{ sync: TallySyncDto | null }>({\n 45\t    queryKey: tallyKeys.latestSync(companyId ?? \"\"),\n 46\t    queryFn: () => tallyApi.latestSync(companyId!),\n 47\t    enabled: isBrowser && !!companyId,\n 48\t    staleTime: 30_000,\n 49\t  });\n 50\t}\n 51\t\n 52\texport function useTallySync() {\n 53\t  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\n 54\t  return useMutation<TallySyncDto, Error, { companyId: string; tallyCompanyName: string }>({\n 55\t    mutationFn: ({ companyId, tallyCompanyName }) => tallyApi.sync(companyId, tallyCompanyName),\n 56\t    onSuccess: (data) => {\n 57\t      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: tallyKeys.latestSync(data.company_id) });\n 58\t      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [\"ledgers\"] });\n 59\t    },\n 60\t  });\n 61\t}\n 62\t\n 63\texport function useTallyMappingSync() {\n 64\t  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\n 65\t  return useMutation<TallyMappingSyncDto, Error, { companyId: string; tallyCompanyName: string }>({\n 66\t    mutationFn: ({ companyId, tallyCompanyName }) =>\n 67\t      tallyApi.syncAccountingMappings(companyId, tallyCompanyName),\n 68\t    onSuccess: () => {\n 69\t      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [\"mappings\"] });\n 70\t      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [\"vouchers\"] });\n 71\t      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [\"invoices\"] });\n 72\t    },\n 73\t  });\n 74\t}\n 75\t\n 76\texport function useTallyBankSync() {\n 77\t  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\n 78\t  return useMutation<TallyBankSyncDto, Error, { companyId: string; tallyCompanyName: string }>({\n 79\t    mutationFn: ({ companyId, tallyCompanyName }) =>\n 80\t      tallyApi.syncBankAccounts(companyId, tallyCompanyName),\n 81\t    onSuccess: () => {\n 82\t      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [\"bankAccounts\"] });\n 83\t    },\n 84\t  });\n 85\t}\n 86\t\n 87\texport function useTallyBankDataSync() {\n 88\t  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\n 89\t  return useMutation<\n 90\t    TallyBankDataSyncDto,\n 91\t    Error,\n 92\t    { companyId: string; tallyCompanyName: string; fromDate?: string; toDate?: string }\n 93\t  >({\n 94\t    mutationFn: ({ companyId, tallyCompanyName, fromDate, toDate }) =>\n 95\t      tallyApi.syncBankData(companyId, tallyCompanyName, fromDate, toDate),\n 96\t    onSuccess: () => {\n 97\t      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [\"bankAccounts\"] });\n 98\t      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [\"bankStatements\"] });\n 99\t    },\n100\t  });\n101\t}","bytes":3370,"startLine":1,"endLine":101,"totalLines":101}

{"content":"  1\t\"\"\"AI-assisted ledger selection, used only as a last resort.\n  2\t\n  3\tThe deterministic chain in `app.services.ledger_suggestion` is always tried\n  4\tfirst: approved mappings, approved voucher history, then configured vendor and\n  5\tcompany ledgers. When none of those yield a ledger the reviewer would otherwise\n  6\tface an empty dropdown, so this module asks the configured AI provider to pick\n  7\tone.\n  8\t\n  9\tTwo rules keep the result honest:\n 10\t\n 11\t* the provider chooses from the company's existing active ledgers by exact name\n 12\t  and nothing else, so no ledger master record is ever invented; and\n 13\t* the choice is returned with `source=\"AI_SUGGESTION\"` and always carries a\n 14\t  review exception, so the voucher lands in `NEEDS_REVIEW` and a person\n 15\t  confirms or changes it before approval.\n 16\t\n 17\tA missing API key, an unparseable answer or a name that is not in the master\n 18\tlist all resolve to \"no suggestion\" rather than a guess.\n 19\t\"\"\"\n 20\t\n 21\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n 22\t\n 23\timport json\n 24\timport logging\n 25\timport re\n 26\timport uuid\n 27\tfrom decimal import Decimal\n 28\t\n 29\tfrom sqlalchemy.orm import Session\n 30\t\n 31\tfrom app.models.invoice import Invoice\n 32\tfrom app.models.invoice_line_item import InvoiceLineItem\n 33\tfrom app.models.ledger import Ledger\n 34\tfrom app.schemas.voucher import LedgerSuggestion\n 35\tfrom app.services.ai import AIProviderError, get_ai_provider\n 36\tfrom app.services.ledger import is_posting_head_candidate\n 37\t\n 38\tlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n 39\t\n 40\t#: Upper bound on ledger names sent to the provider. A company with more active\n 41\t#: ledgers than this gets no AI suggestion rather than a silently truncated\n 42\t#: choice.\n 43\t# shortcut: companies with >300 active ledgers get no AI suggestion, upgrade to\n 44\t# a retrieval step that pre-filters candidates by parent group if that happens.\n 45\t_MAX_CANDIDATES = 300\n 46\t\n 47\t#: Upper bound on invoice line descriptions quoted into the prompt.\n 48\t_MAX_ITEMS = 20\n 49\t\n 50\t_SYSTEM_PROMPT = (\n 51\t    \"You are an Indian accounting assistant. You map a purchase or sales \"\n 52\t    \"invoice to exactly one ledger from a company's Tally ledger master. \"\n 53\t    \"You never invent a ledger name: you may only return a name that appears \"\n 54\t    \"verbatim in the supplied list, or null when none of them fits.\"\n 55\t)\n 56\t\n 57\t\n 58\tdef _candidate_lines(ledgers: list[Ledger]) -> str:\n 59\t    rows = []\n 60\t    for ledger in ledgers:\n 61\t        detail = \" · \".join(\n 62\t            part\n 63\t            for part in (ledger.parent_group, ledger.ledger_type)\n 64\t            if part and part.strip()\n 65\t        )\n 66\t        rows.append(f\"- {ledger.name}\" + (f\" ({detail})\" if detail else \"\"))\n 67\t    return \"\\n\".join(rows)\n 68\t\n 69\t\n 70\tdef _invoice_context(\n 71\t    invoice: Invoice, items: list[InvoiceLineItem], vendor_name: str | None\n 72\t) -> str:\n 73\t    parts = [f\"Vendor/customer: {vendor_name or invoice.vendor_name or 'unknown'}\"]\n 74\t    if invoice.invoice_number:\n 75\t        parts.append(f\"Invoice number: {invoice.invoice_number}\")\n 76\t    if invoice.taxable_amount is not None:\n 77\t        parts.append(f\"Taxable amount: {invoice.taxable_amount}\")\n 78\t    if items:\n 79\t        parts.append(\"Line items:\")\n 80\t        for item in items[:_MAX_ITEMS]:\n\n… 130 more line(s). Read with offset=81 to continue.","bytes":7764,"startLine":1,"endLine":80,"totalLines":210,"nextOffset":81}

You map a purchase or sales \\\"\\n 52\\t    \\\"invoice to exactly one ledger from a company's Tally ledger master. \\\"\\n 53\\t    \\\"You never invent a ledger name: you may only return a name that appears \\\"\\n 54\\t    \\\"verbatim in the supplied list, or null when none of them fits.\\\"\\n 55\\t)\\n 56\\t\\n 57\\t\\n 58\\tdef _candidate_lines(ledgers: list[Ledger]) -> str:\\n 59\\t    rows = []\\n 60\\t    for ledger in ledgers:\\n 61\\t        detail = \\\" · \\\".join(\\n 62\\t            part\\n 63\\t            for part in (ledger.parent_group, ledger.ledger_type)\\n 64\\t            if part and part.strip()\\n 65\\t        )\\n 66\\t        rows.append(f\\\"- {ledger.name}\\\" + (f\\\" ({detail})\\\" if detail else \\\"\\\"))\\n 67\\t    return \\\"\\\\n\\\".join(rows)\\n 68\\t\\n 69\\t\\n 70\\tdef _invoice_context(\\n 71\\t    invoice: Invoice, items: list[InvoiceLineItem], vendor_name: str | None\\n 72\\t) -> str:\\n 73\\t    parts = [f\\\"Vendor/customer: {vendor_name or invoice.vendor_name or 'unknown'}\\\"]\\n 74\\t    if invoice.invoice_number:\\n 75\\t        parts.append(f\\\"Invoice number: {invoice.invoice_number}\\\")\\n 76\\t    if invoice.taxable_amount is not None:\\n 77\\t        parts.append(f\\\"Taxable amount: {invoice.taxable_amount}\\\")\\n 78\\t    if items:\\n 79\\t        parts.append(\\\"Line items:\\\")\\n 80\\t        for item in items[:_MAX_ITEMS]:\\n\\n… 130 more line(s). {"exitCode":0,"stdout":"# Phase 1 Accounting Gap Analysis\n\n## 1. Purpose and scope\n\nThis document records the gap between PAL's pre-existing document/extraction\npipeline and the Phase 1 accounting workflow required by the client's\n`Accounting_Automation_Flowcharts_and_Process.docx` and the three client\nworkbooks. It is the evidence behind the implementation in\n`docs/phase1-accounting-workflow.md`.\n\nPhase 1 covers:\n\n- a company-scoped Ledger Master;\n- explainable ledger suggestions;\n- draft vouchers created from invoices or bank transactions;\n- human review, editing, approval and rejection;\n- an append-only accounting audit trail;\n- an approved-voucher import file whose generation is not a posting;\n- explicit verification before a voucher becomes `POSTED`.\n\nPhase 1 does **not** cover direct Tally posting, Zoho OAuth, advanced AI\naccounting decisions, GST/TDS workings, or GST 2B reconciliation.\n\n## 2. Source of truth\n\nThe following client files are treated as contracts and are not modified:\n\n| Source | Use |\n| --- | --- |\n| `Accounting_Automation_Flowcharts_and_Process.docx` | Required process, statuses and control points. |\n| `Invoice Requirements v.00.xlsx` | Invoice input/output headers and terminology. |\n| `bank upload reference.xlsx` | Bank-voucher workbook sheet names, headers, styles and Read Me. |\n| `AccountingVouchers.xlsx` | Accounting-voucher workbook sheet names, headers, styles and Read Me. |\n\nThe workbooks are output contracts, not PAL's internal data model. Bundled\ngenerator copies live under `backend/app/services/exports/templates/`; the\noriginals in the repository root remain untouched.\n\n## 3. State before Phase 1\n\n### 3.1 What existed\n\n- Text-first document extraction and invoice/bank data capture.\n- Company-scoped records with `companyId` and backend company filters.\n- Vendor Master fields for default expense ledger and creditor ledger.\n- Strict invoice and bank workbook exports with deterministic, idempotent file\n  generation.\n- Client workbook templates and Read Me sheets.\n- Existing modules: Invoice Processing, Bank Statements, Tally Integration.\n\n### 3.2 What was missing\n\n| Gap | Consequence before Phase 1 |\n| --- | --- |\n| No company-scoped ledger master | A ledger name could be typed into a vendor or company field but was not a first-class, validated company record. |\n| No traceable ledger suggestion evidence | There was no machine-readable explanation of why a ledger was proposed. |\n| No draft voucher object | Invoices and bank lines could be extracted and exported, but not reviewed as accounting vouchers. |\n| No explicit voucher lifecycle | There was no enforced distinction between draft, approved, exported and posted. |\n| No approval gate | A file could be generated without a recorded human approval. |\n| No append-only accounting audit | Source values, edits, approvals and export/posting decisions were not preserved as accounting history. |\n=====README=====\n# Welcome to your Lovable project\n\nThis project was built with [Lovable](https://lovable.dev).\n\n## Build with Lovable\n\nOpen your project in the [Lovable editor](https://lovable.dev) and keep building.\n\n- **Ship faster**: describe what you want to build and Lovable handles the code.\n- **Stay in sync**: connect the project to GitHub and every change made in Lovable is committed straight to your repository.\n- **Full ownership**: this code is yours. Push to your repository and your changes sync back into Lovable, ready for your next prompt.\n\n## Development\n\nPrefer working locally? You need Node.js and npm �?\" [install with nvm](https://github.com/nvm-sh/nvm#installing-and-updating).\n\n```sh\ngit clone <this-repository-url>\ncd <repository-name>\nnpm i\nnpm run dev\n```\n\n## Built with\n\n- TanStack Start\n- TypeScript\n- React\n- Tailwind CSS\n","stderr":"","timedOut":false,"cancelled":false,"cleanup":{"status":"observed","reason":"The owned Windows Job has no processes and the exact target handle is closed."},"verificationStatus":"unverified","verificationBasis":"command_exit","verificationNote":"Unverified: command completed without a recognized check summary or valid actual/expected receipt."}

Purpose and scope\\n\\nThis document records the gap between PAL's pre-existing document/extraction\\npipeline and the Phase 1 accounting workflow required by the client's\\n`Accounting_Automation_Flowcharts_and_Process.docx` and the three client\\nworkbooks. It is the evidence behind the implementation in\\n`docs/phase1-accounting-workflow.md`.\\n\\nPhase 1 covers:\\n\\n- a company-scoped Ledger Master;\\n- explainable ledger suggestions;\\n- draft vouchers created from invoices or bank transactions;\\n- human review, editing, approval and rejection;\\n- an append-only accounting audit trail;\\n- an approved-voucher import file whose generation is not a posting;\\n- explicit verification before a voucher becomes `POSTED`.\\n\\nPhase 1 does **not** cover direct Tally posting, Zoho OAuth, advanced AI\\naccounting decisions, GST/TDS workings, or GST 2B reconciliation.\\n\\n## 2. Source of truth\\n\\nThe following client files are treated as contracts and are not modified:\\n\\n| Source | Use |\\n| --- | --- |\\n| `Accounting_Automation_Flowcharts_and_Process.docx` | Required process, statuses and control points. |\\n| `Invoice Requirements v.00.xlsx` | Invoice input/output headers and terminology. |\\n| `bank upload reference.xlsx` | Bank-voucher workbook sheet names, headers, styles and Read Me. |\\n| `AccountingVouchers.xlsx` | Accounting-voucher workbook sheet names, headers, styles and Read Me. |\\n\\nThe workbooks are output contracts, not PAL's internal data model. Bundled\\ngenerator copies live under `backend/app/services/exports/templates/`; the\\noriginals in the repository root remain untouched.\\n\\n## 3. State before Phase 1\\n\\n### 3.1 What existed\\n\\n- Text-first document extraction and invoice/bank data capture.\\n- Company-scoped records with `companyId` and backend company filters.\\n- Vendor Master fields for default expense ledger and creditor ledger.\\n- Strict invoice and bank workbook exports with deterministic, idempotent file\\n  generation.\\n- Client workbook templates and Read Me sheets.\\n- Existing modules: Invoice Processing, Bank Statements, Tally Integration.\\n\\n### 3.2 What was missing\\n\\n| Gap | Consequence before Phase 1 |\\n| --- | --- |\\n| No company-scoped ledger master | A ledger name could be typed into a vendor or company field but was not a first-class, validated company record. |\\n| No traceable ledger suggestion evidence | There was no machine-readable explanation of why a ledger was proposed. |\\n| No draft voucher object | Invoices and bank lines could be extracted and exported, but not reviewed as accounting vouchers. |\\n| No explicit voucher lifecycle | There was no enforced distinction between draft, approved, exported and posted. |\\n| No approval gate | A file could be generated without a recorded human approval. |\\n| No append-only accounting audit | Source values, edits, approvals and export/posting decisions were not preserved as accounting history. |\\n=====README=====\\n# Welcome to your Lovable project\\n\\nThis project was built with [Lovable](https://lovable.dev).\\n\\n## Build with Lovable\\n\\nOpen your project in the [Lovable editor](https://lovable.dev) and keep building.\\n\\n- **Ship faster**: describe what you want to build and Lovable handles the code.\\n- **Stay in sync**: connect the project to GitHub and every change made in Lovable is committed straight to your repository.\\n- **Full ownership**: this code is yours. Push to your repository and your changes sync back into Lovable, ready for your next prompt.\\n\\n## Development\\n\\nPrefer working locally? You need Node.js and npm �?\\\" [install with nvm](https://github.com/nvm-sh/nvm#installing-and-updating).\\n\\n```sh\\ngit clone <this-repository-url>\\ncd <repository-name>\\nnpm i\\nnpm run dev\\n```\\n\\n## Built with\\n\\n- TanStack Start\\n- TypeScript\\n- React\\n- Tailwind CSS\\n\",\"stderr\":\"\",\"timedOut\":false,\"cancelled\":false,\"cleanup\":{\"status\":\"observed\",\"reason\":\"The owned Windows Job has no processes and the exact target handle is closed.\"},\"verificationStatus\":\"unverified\",\"verificationBasis\":\"command_exit\",\"verificationNote\":\"Unverified: command completed without a recognized check summary or valid actual/expected receipt.\"}\n[trace revision=67 evidence=E0052]\n[trace writeRevision=67]\n\n[Outcome not yet verified for exec] The actual result above is execution evidence, not proof of the requested outcome.
